``` ts
// src/types/note.ts
// 笔记相关公共类型：api 层与 store 层共用，避免循环引用

// 笔记页内部的子块（支持普通文本块、可折叠代码块、内嵌图片块混排）
export interface NoteSubBlock {
  id: string;
  type: 'text' | 'code' | 'image';
  content: string;
  language: string;
  collapsed: boolean;
  title: string;
  imageUrl?: string;
  imageWidth?: number; // 百分比宽度：25 | 50 | 75 | 100
}

// 统一页面结构：可以是笔记页 ('note'，使用 richContent 富文本 HTML) 或代码页 ('code')
export interface CodePage {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  pageType: 'note' | 'code';
  language: string;
  code: string;
  blocks: NoteSubBlock[];
  richContent?: string;
}

export interface NoteFolder {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface NoteItem {
  id: string;
  folderId: string | null;
  title: string;
  content: string;
  codePages: CodePage[];
  isPublic?: boolean;
  isOwner?: boolean;
  authorName?: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  version?: string;
}
```

### >>> FILE START [03/12]: src/utils/api.ts <<< ###
``` ts
// src/utils/api.ts
import type { CodePage, NoteFolder, NoteItem } from '../types/note';

// 所有接口一律使用以 /api 开头的相对路径：
// - 开发环境由 vite.config.ts 的 server.proxy 转发到远程服务器
// - 生产环境由 Nginx 自动转发到后端
// 这里固定为空字符串，绝不允许出现 http://localhost 之类的绝对地址
export const API_BASE_URL = '';

function getAuthHeaders(): HeadersInit {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
  return {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {})
  };
}

// 携带 HTTP 状态码与后端 detail 的请求异常，便于调用方做差异化提示
export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;
  detail?: string;

  constructor(message: string, status: number, detail?: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
    this.status = status;
    this.detail = detail;
  }
}

async function handleResponse<T>(res: Response): Promise<T> {
  if (!res.ok) {
    const errorData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const detail = typeof errorData?.detail === 'string' ? errorData.detail : undefined;
    throw new ApiError(detail || `请求失败: HTTP ${res.status}`, res.status, detail);
  }
  return res.json();
}

export const api = {
  // === 认证接口 ===
  auth: {
    login: (username: string, password: string) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/login`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ username, password })
      }).then(handleResponse<{ access_token: string; user: Record<string, unknown> }>),

    getMe: () =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/me`, {
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(handleResponse<Record<string, unknown>>),

    changePassword: (new_password: string) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/change-password`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ new_password })
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string }>),

    updateTheme: (theme: 'dark' | 'light' | 'eyecare') =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/theme`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ theme })
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string; theme: 'dark' | 'light' | 'eyecare' }>),

    updateNoteAutoSave: (note_auto_save: boolean) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/note-auto-save`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ note_auto_save })
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string; noteAutoSave: boolean }>),

    getUsers: () =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/users`, {
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(handleResponse<Array<Record<string, unknown>>>),

    createUser: (username: string, name: string, allowed_projects: string[]) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/users`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ username, name, allowed_projects })
      }).then(handleResponse<{ user: Record<string, unknown>; initialPassword: string }>),

    deleteUser: (userId: string) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/users/${userId}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string }>)
  },

  // === 文件与目录操作接口 ===
  files: {
    // 获取真实存储空间统计（区分文件占用与笔记占用）
    getStorageStats: () =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/files/storage-stats`, {
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(
        handleResponse<{
          filesBytes: number;
          notesBytes: number;
          usedBytes: number;
          totalQuotaBytes: number;
        }>
      ),

    list: (is_public: boolean, sub_path: string = '') => {
      const params = new URLSearchParams({ is_public: String(is_public), sub_path });
      return fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/files/list?${params.toString()}`, {
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(handleResponse<Array<Record<string, unknown>>>);
    },

    createFolder: (name: string, parent_path: string, is_public: boolean) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/files/folder`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ name, parent_path, is_public })
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string }>),

    rename: (virtual_path: string, new_name: string, is_public: boolean) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/files/rename`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ virtual_path, new_name, is_public })
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string }>),

    // 支持单文件或批量跨区移动
    move: (req: {
      source_virtual_path?: string;
      source_virtual_paths?: string[];
      target_virtual_path: string;
      source_is_public: boolean;
      target_is_public: boolean;
    }) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/files/move`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify(req)
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string; moved_count?: number }>),

    // 支持单文件或批量复制（同名自动追加 (1) 后缀）
    copy: (req: {
      source_virtual_paths: string[];
      target_virtual_path: string;
      source_is_public: boolean;
      target_is_public: boolean;
    }) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/files/copy`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify(req)
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string; copied_names?: string[] }>),

    delete: (virtual_path: string, is_public: boolean) => {
      const params = new URLSearchParams({ virtual_path, is_public: String(is_public) });
      return fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/files/delete?${params.toString()}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string }>);
    },

    // 批量删除接口
    batchDelete: (virtual_paths: string[], is_public: boolean) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/files/batch-delete`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ virtual_paths, is_public })
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string; deleted_count?: number }>),

    // 获取可共享的用户列表
    getShareableUsers: () =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/files/shareable-users`, {
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(handleResponse<Array<{ id: string; username: string; name: string; role: string }>>),

    // 设置单文件或批量文件定向共享名单（shared_user_ids 包含 'ALL' 代表所有人及访客可见）
    setShare: (data: { virtual_path?: string; virtual_paths?: string[]; shared_user_ids: string[] }) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/files/share`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify(data)
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string }>),

    getDownloadUrl: (virtual_path: string, is_public: boolean) => {
      const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token') || '';
      const params = new URLSearchParams({ virtual_path, is_public: String(is_public) });
      return `${API_BASE_URL}/api/files/download?${params.toString()}&token=${token}`;
    },

    // 真实分片上传与落盘（新增 relativePath 参数以保持完整文件夹层级结构）
    uploadFileWithChunks: async (
      file: File,
      parentPath: string,
      isPublic: boolean,
      onProgress: (percent: number) => void,
      relativePath?: string
    ) => {
      const CHUNK_SIZE = 2 * 1024 * 1024; // 2MB 一个分片
      const totalChunks = Math.ceil(file.size / CHUNK_SIZE) || 1;
      const uploadId = `upl_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 9)}`;
      const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');

      for (let i = 0; i < totalChunks; i++) {
        const start = i * CHUNK_SIZE;
        const end = Math.min(file.size, start + CHUNK_SIZE);
        const chunkBlob = file.slice(start, end);

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('upload_id', uploadId);
        formData.append('chunk_index', String(i));
        formData.append('file', chunkBlob, file.name);

        const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/files/upload-chunk`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
          body: formData
        });
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`分片 ${i} 上传失败`);

        const percent = Math.round(((i + 1) / totalChunks) * 100);
        onProgress(percent);
      }

      // 所有分片落盘后，触发合并接口
      return fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/files/merge-chunks`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({
          upload_id: uploadId,
          filename: file.name,
          relative_path: relativePath || file.name,
          parent_path: parentPath,
          is_public: isPublic,
          total_chunks: totalChunks
        })
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string }>);
    }
  },

  // === 云端笔记接口 ===
  notes: {
    getVersion: () =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/notes/version`, {
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(handleResponse<{ version: string }>),

    getAll: () =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/notes/all`, {
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(handleResponse<{ folders: NoteFolder[]; notes: NoteItem[]; version?: string }>),

    createFolder: (name: string) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/notes/folder`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ name })
      }).then(handleResponse<NoteFolder>),

    deleteFolder: (folderId: string) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/notes/folder/${folderId}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string }>),

    save: (data: {
      id?: string;
      folder_id: string | null;
      title: string;
      content: string;
      code_pages: CodePage[];
      is_public?: boolean;
    }) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/notes/save`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify(data)
      }).then(handleResponse<NoteItem>),

    saveKeepalive: (data: {
      id?: string;
      folder_id: string | null;
      title: string;
      content: string;
      code_pages: CodePage[];
      is_public?: boolean;
    }) => {
      try {
        fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/notes/save`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: getAuthHeaders(),
          body: JSON.stringify(data),
          keepalive: true
        });
      } catch {
        // 忽略关闭页面时的中断异常
      }
    },

    // 切换笔记的公开/私密状态
    togglePublic: (note_id: string, is_public: boolean) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/notes/toggle-public`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ note_id, is_public })
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string; isPublic: boolean; version?: string }>),

    updateShares: (data: {
      note_id: string;
      is_public?: boolean;
      shares: Array<{ user_id: string; permission: 'read' | 'edit' }>;
    }) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/notes/shares`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify(data)
      }).then(
        handleResponse<{
          message: string;
          isPublic: boolean;
          shares: Array<{ userId: string; username: string; name: string; permission: 'read' | 'edit' }>;
          version?: string;
        }>
      ),

    // 上传笔记内嵌图片（粘贴截图或本地图片）
    uploadImage: async (file: File | Blob, filename = 'paste.png') => {
      const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('file', file, filename);
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/notes/upload-image`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
        body: formData
      });
      return handleResponse<{ url: string; filename: string }>(res);
    },

    deleteNote: (noteId: string) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/notes/item/${noteId}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string }>)
  },

  // === 实时屏幕共享接口 ===
  screen: {
    getIceConfig: () =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/screen/ice-config`, {
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(handleResponse<{ iceServers: RTCIceServer[] }>),

    listRooms: () =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/screen/rooms`, {
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(
        handleResponse<
          Array<{
            roomId: string;
            hostName: string;
            hostUsername: string;
            allowMode: 'code' | 'users';
            qualityLabel: string;
            createdAt: string;
            isSelfDevice: boolean;
            viewerCount: number;
          }>
        >
      ),

    createRoom: (data: {
      allow_mode: 'code' | 'users';
      allowed_user_ids: string[];
      quality_label: string;
    }) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/screen/rooms`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify(data)
      }).then(
        handleResponse<{
          roomId: string;
          clientId: string;
          wsTicket: string;
          allowMode: 'code' | 'users';
          qualityLabel: string;
          createdAt: string;
        }>
      ),

    joinRoom: (roomId: string) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/screen/rooms/${encodeURIComponent(roomId)}/join`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(
        handleResponse<{
          roomId: string;
          clientId: string;
          wsTicket: string;
          hostClientId: string;
          hostName: string;
          hostUsername: string;
          qualityLabel: string;
        }>
      ),

    closeRoom: (roomId: string) =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/screen/rooms/${encodeURIComponent(roomId)}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(handleResponse<{ message: string }>),

    getWsUrl: (roomId: string, ticket: string) => {
      const protocol = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
      return `${protocol}//${window.location.host}/api/screen/ws/${encodeURIComponent(roomId)}?ticket=${encodeURIComponent(ticket)}`;
    }
  },

  // === 审计接口 ===
  audit: {
    getLogs: () =>
      fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/audit/logs`, {
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      }).then(handleResponse<Array<Record<string, unknown>>>)
  }
};
```

### >>> FILE START [04/12]: src/store/useAuthStore.ts <<< ###
``` ts
﻿// src/store/useAuthStore.ts
import { create } from 'zustand';
import {
  Sparkles,
  Box,
  Shield,
  Activity,
  Hexagon,
  Layers,
  Zap,
  Star,
  Flame,
  Compass,
  Target,
  type LucideIcon,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '../utils/api';

export type UserRole = 'admin' | 'user' | 'guest';
export type ThemeMode = 'dark' | 'light' | 'eyecare' | (string & {});

export const LOGO_ICONS = [
  'ring',
  'sparkle',
  'cube',
  'shield',
  'pulse',
  'hexagon',
  'layers',
  'zap',
  'star',
  'flame',
  'compass',
  'target',
] as const;
export type LogoIcon = (typeof LOGO_ICONS)[number];

export const LOGO_ICON_LABELS: Record<LogoIcon, string> = {
  ring: '空心圆环',
  sparkle: '星芒',
  cube: '立方',
  shield: '盾牌',
  pulse: '脉冲',
  hexagon: '六边形',
  layers: '层叠',
  zap: '闪电',
  star: '星形',
  flame: '火焰',
  compass: '罗盘',
  target: '靶心',
};

export const LOGO_ICON_COMPONENTS: Record<Exclude<LogoIcon, 'ring'>, LucideIcon> = {
  sparkle: Sparkles,
  cube: Box,
  shield: Shield,
  pulse: Activity,
  hexagon: Hexagon,
  layers: Layers,
  zap: Zap,
  star: Star,
  flame: Flame,
  compass: Compass,
  target: Target,
};

export const isLogoIcon = (value: unknown): value is LogoIcon =>
  typeof value === 'string' && (LOGO_ICONS as readonly string[]).includes(value);

export interface ThemeLogo {
  icon: LogoIcon;
  fg: string;
  bg: string;
}

export interface CustomThemeItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  colors: Record<string, string>;
  updatedAt?: string;
  isBuiltin?: boolean;
  logo?: ThemeLogo;
  activeInMenu?: boolean;
  readonly?: boolean;
}

export interface UserAccount {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  name: string;
  role: UserRole;
  password?: string;
  mustChangePassword?: boolean;
  allowedProjects: string[];
  theme?: ThemeMode;
  noteAutoSave?: boolean;
  avatarUrl?: string | null;
  createdAt?: string;
}

interface AuthStore {
  users: UserAccount[];
  currentUser: UserAccount | null;
  theme: ThemeMode;
  customThemes: CustomThemeItem[];
  themes: CustomThemeItem[];
  noteAutoSave: boolean;
  fetchUsers: () => Promise<void>;
  syncCurrentUser: () => Promise<void>;
  setTheme: (theme: ThemeMode) => Promise<void>;
  fetchThemes: () => Promise<void>;
  toggleThemeInMenu: (themeId: string) => void;
  importCustomTheme: (payload: {
    id?: string;
    name: string;
    description?: string;
    logo?: ThemeLogo;
    activeInMenu?: boolean;
    colors: Record<string, string>;
  }) => Promise<void>;
  updateCustomTheme: (
    themeId: string,
    payload: {
      name: string;
      description?: string;
      logo?: ThemeLogo;
      colors: Record<string, string>;
    }
  ) => Promise<void>;
  deleteCustomTheme: (themeId: string) => Promise<void>;
  setNoteAutoSave: (enabled: boolean) => Promise<void>;
  login: (username: string, password: string) => Promise<{ success: boolean; message?: string; mustChange?: boolean }>;
  logout: () => void;
  guestLogin: () => void;
  createUser: (username: string, name: string, allowedProjects: string[]) => Promise<{ user: UserAccount; initialPass: string }>;
  deleteUser: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
  updateUserProjects: (userId: string, allowedProjects: string[]) => Promise<void>;
  setAllowedProjects: (projects: string[]) => void;
  uploadAvatar: (file: File) => Promise<string>;
  resetAvatar: () => Promise<void>;
  changePassword: (newPass: string) => Promise<void>;
}

const VALID_THEMES = ['dark', 'light', 'eyecare'] as const;
type BuiltinTheme = (typeof VALID_THEMES)[number];

const isBuiltinTheme = (val: string): val is BuiltinTheme =>
  (VALID_THEMES as readonly string[]).includes(val);

export const THEME_COLOR_FIELDS = [
  { key: '--bg-base', label: '主背景', description: '页面整体背景色' },
  { key: '--bg-surface', label: '卡片面', description: '主要卡片与面板表面色' },
  { key: '--bg-surface-alt', label: '次卡片', description: '次级卡片与列表项背景色' },
  { key: '--bg-sidebar', label: '侧边栏', description: '左侧导航栏背景色' },
  { key: '--bg-panel', label: '面板区', description: '独立面板区域背景色' },
  { key: '--bg-input', label: '输入框', description: '输入框与文本域背景色' },
  { key: '--bg-hover', label: '悬浮底', description: '鼠标悬浮时的背景色' },
  { key: '--bg-hover-strong', label: '强悬浮', description: '强调悬浮与按钮背景色' },
  { key: '--text-primary', label: '主文字', description: '标题与正文主文字色' },
  { key: '--text-secondary', label: '次文字', description: '次要说明文字色' },
  { key: '--text-muted', label: '弱文字', description: '弱化提示文字色' },
  { key: '--text-subtle', label: '极弱字', description: '占位与极弱提示文字色' },
  { key: '--border-main', label: '主边框', description: '主要分隔线与边框色' },
  { key: '--border-subtle', label: '弱边框', description: '浅色分隔线与边框色' },
  { key: '--chart-free', label: '空闲轨', description: '存储环形图空闲轨道色' },
] as const;

const CUSTOM_THEME_VAR_KEYS = [
  ...THEME_COLOR_FIELDS.map((field) => field.key),
  '--logo-bg',
  '--logo-fg',
];

  '--bg-base': '#1e2128',
  '--bg-surface': '#2a2d35',
  '--bg-surface-alt': '#252830',
  '--bg-sidebar': '#16181d',

const MENU_ACTIVE_KEY = 'app_theme_menu_active';
const BUILTIN_OVERRIDE_KEY = 'app_theme_builtin_overrides';

function readStorageRecord<T>(key: string): Record<string, T> {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
    const parsed = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
    return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' ? parsed : {};
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

function writeStorageRecord<T>(key: string, value: Record<string, T>) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export function getThemeLogo(item: CustomThemeItem | null | undefined): ThemeLogo {
  const legacy = item as (CustomThemeItem & { logoShape?: unknown }) | null | undefined;
  const raw = item?.logo;
  const rawIconValue: unknown = raw?.icon;
  const rawIcon = rawIconValue === 'ai' ? 'ring' : rawIconValue;
  const legacyIcon = legacy?.logoShape === 'ai' ? 'ring' : legacy?.logoShape;
  return {
    icon: isLogoIcon(rawIcon)
      ? rawIcon
      : isLogoIcon(legacyIcon)
        ? legacyIcon
        : 'ring',
    fg: raw?.fg || item?.colors?.['--logo-fg'] || '#ffffff',
    bg: raw?.bg || item?.colors?.['--logo-bg'] || '#2563eb',
  };
}

function stripLegacyLogoColors(colors: Record<string, string>): Record<string, string> {
  const next = { ...(colors || {}) };
  delete next['--logo-border'];
  return next;
}

export function getAllThemes(customThemes: CustomThemeItem[]): CustomThemeItem[] {
  const menuFlags = readStorageRecord<boolean>(MENU_ACTIVE_KEY);
  const overrides = readStorageRecord<Partial<CustomThemeItem>>(BUILTIN_OVERRIDE_KEY);
  const builtins = BUILTIN_THEME_DEFS.map((item) => {
    const override = overrides[item.id];
    const merged: CustomThemeItem = {
      ...item,
      name: override?.name || item.name,
      description: override?.description ?? item.description,
      logo: override?.logo || item.logo,
      colors: stripLegacyLogoColors({ ...item.colors, ...(override?.colors || {}) }),
      activeInMenu: menuFlags[item.id] !== false,
    };
    return { ...merged, logo: getThemeLogo(merged) };
  });

  const seen = new Set<string>(builtins.map((item) => item.id));
  const customs = customThemes
    .filter((item) => Boolean(item && typeof item.id === 'string' && item.id.trim() && !seen.has(item.id)))
    .map((item) => {
      seen.add(item.id);
      const merged: CustomThemeItem = {
        ...item,
        colors: stripLegacyLogoColors(item.colors),
        activeInMenu: menuFlags[item.id] !== false,
      };
      return { ...merged, logo: getThemeLogo(merged) };
    });

  return [...builtins, ...customs];
}

let runtimeAllThemes: CustomThemeItem[] = getAllThemes([]);

export function sanitizeTheme(val: unknown): ThemeMode {
  if (typeof val !== 'string') return 'dark';
  const trimmed = val.trim();
  return trimmed || 'dark';
}

function isLightColor(value: unknown): boolean {
  const hex = String(value || '').trim().replace(/^#/, '');
  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(hex)) return false;
  const r = parseInt(hex.slice(0, 2), 16);
  const g = parseInt(hex.slice(2, 4), 16);
  const b = parseInt(hex.slice(4, 6), 16);
  return 0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b > 180;
}

export function previewThemeFromList(activeThemeId: string, themeList: CustomThemeItem[]) {
  let savedActiveTheme: string | null = null;
  try {
    savedActiveTheme = localStorage.getItem('app_active_theme');
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
  applyThemeToDom(activeThemeId, themeList);
  try {
    if (savedActiveTheme === null) localStorage.removeItem('app_active_theme');
    else localStorage.setItem('app_active_theme', savedActiveTheme);
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export function applyThemeToDom(
  theme: ThemeMode,
  allThemes: CustomThemeItem[] = runtimeAllThemes
) {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return;
  const root = document.documentElement;
  for (const key of CUSTOM_THEME_VAR_KEYS) {
    root.style.removeProperty(key);
  }
  const found = allThemes.find((item) => item.id === theme);
  const appearance: BuiltinTheme = found
    ? found.id === 'light'
      ? 'light'
      : found.id === 'eyecare'
        ? 'eyecare'
        : isLightColor(found.colors?.['--bg-base'])
          ? 'light'
          : 'dark'
    : isBuiltinTheme(theme)
      ? theme
      : 'dark';
  root.setAttribute('data-theme', appearance);
  if (found) {
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(found.colors || {})) {
      if ((CUSTOM_THEME_VAR_KEYS as readonly string[]).includes(key) && typeof value === 'string' && value.trim()) {
        root.style.setProperty(key, value.trim());
      }
    }
    const logo = getThemeLogo(found);
    root.style.setProperty('--logo-fg', logo.fg);
    root.style.setProperty('--logo-bg', logo.bg);
  }
  try {
    localStorage.setItem('app_active_theme', theme);
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

const getUserThemeStorageKey = (user: UserAccount | null): string => {
  if (!user) return 'app_theme_default';
  if (user.role === 'guest') return 'app_theme_guest';
  return `app_theme_${user.id}`;
};

const getUserAutoSaveStorageKey = (user: UserAccount | null): string => {
  if (!user || user.role === 'guest') return 'app_note_autosave_guest';
  return `app_note_autosave_${user.id}`;
};

const getStoredCurrentUser = (): UserAccount | null => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
  const data = localStorage.getItem('app_current_user');
  if (!data) return null;
  try {
    const user = JSON.parse(data) as UserAccount;
    if (user.role !== 'guest' && !token) return null;
    return {
      ...user,
      allowedProjects: Array.isArray(user.allowedProjects) ? user.allowedProjects : ['通用公用库'],
      theme: sanitizeTheme(user.theme),
      noteAutoSave: Boolean(user.noteAutoSave)
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
};

const resolveInitialTheme = (user: UserAccount | null): ThemeMode => {
  if (user) {
    const cachedUserTheme = localStorage.getItem(getUserThemeStorageKey(user));
    const resolved = sanitizeTheme(user.theme || cachedUserTheme || 'dark');
    applyThemeToDom(resolved);
    return resolved;
  }
  const fallback: ThemeMode = 'dark';
  applyThemeToDom(fallback);
  return fallback;
};

const resolveInitialAutoSave = (user: UserAccount | null): boolean => {
  if (!user || user.role === 'guest') return false;
  if (typeof user.noteAutoSave === 'boolean') return user.noteAutoSave;
  return localStorage.getItem(getUserAutoSaveStorageKey(user)) === 'true';
};

const normalizeUser = (user: UserAccount): UserAccount => ({
  ...user,
  allowedProjects: Array.isArray(user.allowedProjects) ? user.allowedProjects : ['通用公用库'],
  theme: sanitizeTheme(user.theme),
  noteAutoSave: Boolean(user.noteAutoSave)
});

const initialUser = getStoredCurrentUser();
const initialTheme = resolveInitialTheme(initialUser);
const initialAutoSave = resolveInitialAutoSave(initialUser);

export const useAuthStore = create<AuthStore>((set, get) => ({
  users: [],
  currentUser: initialUser,
  theme: initialTheme,
  customThemes: [],
  themes: getAllThemes([]),
  noteAutoSave: initialAutoSave,

  fetchUsers: async () => {
    const list = await api.auth.getUsers();
    set({ users: (list || []).map((user) => normalizeUser(user as unknown as UserAccount)) });
  },

  syncCurrentUser: async () => {
    const state = get();
    if (!state.currentUser || state.currentUser.role === 'guest') return;
    try {
      const me = await api.auth.getMe();
      const normalized = normalizeUser(me as unknown as UserAccount);
      localStorage.setItem('app_current_user', JSON.stringify(normalized));
      const nextTheme = sanitizeTheme(normalized.theme || state.theme);
      applyThemeToDom(nextTheme, get().themes);
      set({
        currentUser: normalized,
        theme: nextTheme,
        noteAutoSave: Boolean(normalized.noteAutoSave)
      });
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  },

  setTheme: async (nextTheme: ThemeMode) => {
    const safe = sanitizeTheme(nextTheme);
    const allThemes = get().themes;
    const isCustom = allThemes.some((item) => item.id === safe && !item.isBuiltin);
    applyThemeToDom(safe, allThemes);
    set({ theme: safe });
    const current = get().currentUser;
    if (current) {
      localStorage.setItem(getUserThemeStorageKey(current), safe);
      if (current.role !== 'guest') {
        const updated = { ...current, theme: safe };
        localStorage.setItem('app_current_user', JSON.stringify(updated));
        set({ currentUser: updated });
        if (!isCustom && isBuiltinTheme(safe)) {
          try {
            await api.auth.updateTheme(safe);
          } catch {
            // ignore
          }
        }
      }
    }
  },

  fetchThemes: async () => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/themes', {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
      });
      if (!res.ok) return;
      const data = await res.json();
      const list = (Array.isArray(data.themes) ? data.themes : []) as CustomThemeItem[];
      const all = getAllThemes(list);
      runtimeAllThemes = all;
      set({ customThemes: list, themes: all });
      applyThemeToDom(get().theme, all);
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  },

  toggleThemeInMenu: (themeId) => {
    const flags = readStorageRecord<boolean>(MENU_ACTIVE_KEY);
    flags[themeId] = flags[themeId] === false;
    writeStorageRecord(MENU_ACTIVE_KEY, flags);
    const all = getAllThemes(get().customThemes);
    runtimeAllThemes = all;
    set({ themes: all });
  },

  importCustomTheme: async (payload) => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
    const res = await fetch('/api/themes/import', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {})
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    });
    const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || '导入风格失败');
    await get().fetchThemes();
  },

  updateCustomTheme: async (themeId, payload) => {
    if (isBuiltinTheme(themeId)) {
      const def = runtimeAllThemes.find((item) => item.id === themeId);
      if (!def || def.readonly) throw new Error('该内置主题方案为只读，无法修改');
      const overrides = readStorageRecord<Partial<CustomThemeItem>>(BUILTIN_OVERRIDE_KEY);
      overrides[themeId] = {
        name: payload.name,
        description: payload.description,
        colors: payload.colors,
        logo: payload.logo,
      };
      writeStorageRecord(BUILTIN_OVERRIDE_KEY, overrides);
      const all = getAllThemes(get().customThemes);
      runtimeAllThemes = all;
      set({ themes: all });
      applyThemeToDom(get().theme, all);
      return;
    }
    const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
    const res = await fetch(`/api/themes/${encodeURIComponent(themeId)}`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {})
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    });
    const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || '更新风格失败');
    await get().fetchThemes();
  },

  deleteCustomTheme: async (themeId) => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
    const res = await fetch(`/api/themes/${encodeURIComponent(themeId)}`, {
      method: 'DELETE',
      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}
    });
    const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || '删除风格失败');
    const nextList = get().customThemes.filter((item) => item.id !== themeId);
    const current = get().currentUser;
    const currentWasCustom = get().theme === themeId;
    const nextTheme: ThemeMode = currentWasCustom ? 'dark' : get().theme;
    runtimeAllThemes = getAllThemes(nextList);
    if (current && current.theme === themeId) {
      const updated = { ...current, theme: 'dark' as ThemeMode };
      localStorage.setItem('app_current_user', JSON.stringify(updated));
      localStorage.setItem(getUserThemeStorageKey(current), 'dark');
      set({ currentUser: updated });
    }
    set({ customThemes: nextList, themes: runtimeAllThemes, theme: nextTheme });
    applyThemeToDom(nextTheme, runtimeAllThemes);
  },

  setNoteAutoSave: async (enabled: boolean) => {
    const current = get().currentUser;
    set({ noteAutoSave: enabled });
    if (current) {
      localStorage.setItem(getUserAutoSaveStorageKey(current), String(enabled));
      if (current.role !== 'guest') {
        const updated = { ...current, noteAutoSave: enabled };
        localStorage.setItem('app_current_user', JSON.stringify(updated));
        set({ currentUser: updated });
        try {
          await api.auth.updateNoteAutoSave(enabled);
        } catch {
          // ignore
        }
      }
    }
  },

  login: async (username: string, password: string) => {
    try {
      const res = await api.auth.login(username, password);
      localStorage.setItem('access_token', res.access_token);
      const normalized = normalizeUser(res.user as unknown as UserAccount);
      localStorage.setItem('app_current_user', JSON.stringify(normalized));
      const nextTheme = sanitizeTheme(normalized.theme || 'dark');
      applyThemeToDom(nextTheme, get().themes);
      set({
        currentUser: normalized,
        theme: nextTheme,
        noteAutoSave: Boolean(normalized.noteAutoSave)
      });
      return { success: true, mustChange: Boolean(normalized.mustChangePassword) };
    } catch (err) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : '登录失败'
      };
    }
  },

  logout: () => {
    localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
    localStorage.removeItem('app_current_user');
    set({ currentUser: null, users: [] });
  },

  guestLogin: () => {
    const guestUser: UserAccount = {
      id: 'guest',
      username: 'guest',
      name: '访客用户',
      role: 'guest',
      allowedProjects: ['通用公用库'],
      theme: 'dark',
      noteAutoSave: false
    };
    localStorage.setItem('app_current_user', JSON.stringify(guestUser));
    applyThemeToDom('dark', get().themes);
    set({ currentUser: guestUser, theme: 'dark', noteAutoSave: false });
  },

  createUser: async (username: string, name: string, allowedProjects: string[]) => {
    const created = (await api.auth.createUser(username, name, allowedProjects)) as Record<string, unknown>;
    const normalized = normalizeUser((created.user || created) as UserAccount);
    const initialPass = String(created.initialPass || created.initial_pass || created.initialPassword || '');
    set((state) => ({ users: [...state.users, normalized] }));
    return { user: normalized, initialPass };
  },

  deleteUser: async (id: string) => {
    await api.auth.deleteUser(id);
    set((state) => ({ users: state.users.filter((u) => u.id !== id) }));
  },

  updateUserProjects: async (userId: string, allowedProjects: string[]) => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
    const res = await fetch(`/api/auth/users/${encodeURIComponent(userId)}/projects`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {})
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ allowed_projects: allowedProjects })
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
      const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      throw new Error(err.detail || '更新成员项目权限失败');
    }
    const data = await res.json();
    const nextList = Array.isArray(data.allowedProjects) ? data.allowedProjects : allowedProjects;
    set((state) => {
      const nextUsers = state.users.map((u) =>
        u.id === userId ? { ...u, allowedProjects: nextList } : u
      );
      const nextCurrent =
        state.currentUser && state.currentUser.id === userId
          ? { ...state.currentUser, allowedProjects: nextList }
          : state.currentUser;
      if (nextCurrent) {
        localStorage.setItem('app_current_user', JSON.stringify(nextCurrent));
      }
      return { users: nextUsers, currentUser: nextCurrent };
    });
  },

  setAllowedProjects: (projects: string[]) => {
    set((state) => {
      if (!state.currentUser) return state;
      const nextCurrent = { ...state.currentUser, allowedProjects: projects };
      localStorage.setItem('app_current_user', JSON.stringify(nextCurrent));
      return { currentUser: nextCurrent };
    });
  },

  uploadAvatar: async (file: File) => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('file', file);
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/avatar', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
      body: formData
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
      const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      throw new Error(err.detail || '上传头像失败');
    }
    const data = await res.json();
    const nextUrl = String(data.avatarUrl || '');
    set((state) => {
      if (!state.currentUser) return state;
      const uid = state.currentUser.id;
      const nextUsers = state.users.map((u) =>
        u.id === uid ? { ...u, avatarUrl: nextUrl } : u
      );
      const nextCurrent = { ...state.currentUser, avatarUrl: nextUrl };
      localStorage.setItem('app_current_user', JSON.stringify(nextCurrent));
      return { users: nextUsers, currentUser: nextCurrent };
    });
    return nextUrl;
  },

  resetAvatar: async () => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('access_token');
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/avatar', {
      method: 'DELETE',
      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
      const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      throw new Error(err.detail || '重置头像失败');
    }
    set((state) => {
      if (!state.currentUser) return state;
      const uid = state.currentUser.id;
      const nextUsers = state.users.map((u) =>
        u.id === uid ? { ...u, avatarUrl: null } : u
      );
      const nextCurrent = { ...state.currentUser, avatarUrl: null };
      localStorage.setItem('app_current_user', JSON.stringify(nextCurrent));
      return { users: nextUsers, currentUser: nextCurrent };
    });
  },

  changePassword: async (newPass: string) => {
    await api.auth.changePassword(newPass);
    const current = get().currentUser;
    if (current) {
      const updated = { ...current, mustChangePassword: false };
      localStorage.setItem('app_current_user', JSON.stringify(updated));
      set({ currentUser: updated });
    }
  }
}));
```

``` ts
// src/store/useNoteStore.ts
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { api } from '../utils/api';

// 旧版笔记子块结构（保留以兼容存量笔记和旧备份包无损迁移）
export interface NoteSubBlock {
  id: string;
  type: 'text' | 'code' | 'image';
  content: string;
  language: string;
  collapsed: boolean;
  title: string;
  imageUrl?: string;
  imageWidth?: number;
}

// 统一页面结构：笔记页 ('note'，使用 richContent 富文本 HTML) 或代码页 ('code')
export interface CodePage {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  pageType: 'note' | 'code';
  language: string;
  code: string;
  blocks: NoteSubBlock[];
  richContent?: string;
}

export interface NoteFolder {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface NoteShareItem {
  userId: string;
  username?: string;
  name?: string;
  permission: 'read' | 'edit';
}

export interface NoteItem {
  id: string;
  folderId: string | null;
  title: string;
  content: string;
  codePages: CodePage[];
  isPublic?: boolean;
  isOwner?: boolean;
  canEdit?: boolean;
  sharePermission?: 'owner' | 'edit' | 'read' | 'public';
  shares?: NoteShareItem[];
  authorName?: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  version?: string;
}

export function formatDefaultNoteTitle(): string {
  const now = new Date();
  const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  return `Note_${now.getFullYear()}年${pad(now.getMonth() + 1)}月${pad(now.getDate())}日_${pad(now.getHours())}点${pad(now.getMinutes())}分${pad(now.getSeconds())}秒`;
}

function escapeHtmlText(str: string): string {
  return (str || '')
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
}

function escapeHtmlAttr(str: string): string {
  return (str || '')
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/\n/g, '&#10;');
}

function formatLegacyInline(text: string): string {
  let html = escapeHtmlText(text);
  html = html.replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
  return html;
}

// 将旧版 blocks 数组（文本/折叠代码窗/图片）无损转换为新版 TipTap 富文本 HTML
export function blocksToRichHtml(blocks: NoteSubBlock[]): string {
  if (!Array.isArray(blocks) || blocks.length === 0) return '<p></p>';
  const parts: string[] = [];

  for (const blk of blocks) {
    if (blk.type === 'code') {
      parts.push(
        `<div data-type="collapsible-code-block" data-code="${escapeHtmlAttr(blk.content || '')}" data-language="${escapeHtmlAttr(blk.language || 'python')}" data-title="${escapeHtmlAttr(blk.title || '代码片段.py')}" data-collapsed="${String(Boolean(blk.collapsed))}"></div>`
      );
    } else if (blk.type === 'image' && blk.imageUrl) {
      parts.push(
        `<div data-type="collapsible-image-block" data-src="${escapeHtmlAttr(blk.imageUrl)}" data-title="${escapeHtmlAttr(blk.title || 'image.png')}" data-width="${Number(blk.imageWidth) || 75}" data-collapsed="${String(Boolean(blk.collapsed))}"></div>`
      );
    } else if (blk.type === 'text') {
      const content = blk.content || '';
      if (!content) continue;
      const lines = content.split('\n');
      for (const line of lines) {
        if (line.startsWith('### ')) {
          parts.push(`<h3>${formatLegacyInline(line.slice(4))}</h3>`);
        } else if (line.startsWith('## ')) {
          parts.push(`<h2>${formatLegacyInline(line.slice(3))}</h2>`);
        } else if (line.startsWith('# ')) {
          parts.push(`<h1>${formatLegacyInline(line.slice(2))}</h1>`);
        } else {
          parts.push(`<p>${formatLegacyInline(line)}</p>`);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return parts.length > 0 ? parts.join('') : '<p></p>';
}

function parseLegacyTextToBlocks(rawText: string): NoteSubBlock[] {
  if (!rawText || !rawText.trim()) {
    return [{ id: `blk_${Date.now()}_0`, type: 'text', content: '', language: 'python', collapsed: false, title: '' }];
  }
  const parts = rawText.split(/```(\w*)\n([\s\S]*?)```/g);
  const blocks: NoteSubBlock[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i += 3) {
    const textSeg = parts[i];
    const langSeg = parts[i + 1];
    const codeSeg = parts[i + 2];

    blocks.push({
      id: `blk_${Date.now()}_${i}`,
      type: 'text',
      content: textSeg || '',
      language: 'python',
      collapsed: false,
      title: ''
    });

    if (codeSeg !== undefined) {
      blocks.push({
        id: `blk_${Date.now()}_${i + 1}`,
        type: 'code',
        content: codeSeg,
        language: langSeg || 'python',
        collapsed: false,
        title: '代码片段'
      });
    }
  }
  return blocks.length > 0
    ? blocks
    : [{ id: `blk_${Date.now()}_0`, type: 'text', content: rawText, language: 'python', collapsed: false, title: '' }];
}

export function normalizeNoteItem(raw: unknown): NoteItem {
  const src = (raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? raw : {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
  const asString = (value: unknown, fallback = ''): string =>
    typeof value === 'string' ? value : fallback;
  const rawPages = Array.isArray(src.codePages)
    ? (src.codePages as Array<Record<string, unknown>>)
    : [];
  const normalizedPages: CodePage[] = [];

  const content = asString(src.content);
  const hasNotePageAlready = rawPages.some((p) => p.pageType === 'note');
  if (content.trim() && !hasNotePageAlready) {
    const legacyBlocks = parseLegacyTextToBlocks(content);
    normalizedPages.push({
      id: `np_legacy_${asString(src.id)}`,
      title: '笔记正文',
      pageType: 'note',
      language: 'markdown',
      code: '',
      blocks: legacyBlocks,
      richContent: blocksToRichHtml(legacyBlocks)
    });
  }

  for (let idx = 0; idx < rawPages.length; idx++) {
    const p = rawPages[idx];
    const pType: 'note' | 'code' = p.pageType === 'note' ? 'note' : 'code';
    const blocks: NoteSubBlock[] =
      pType === 'note'
        ? Array.isArray(p.blocks) && p.blocks.length > 0
          ? (p.blocks as NoteSubBlock[])
          : parseLegacyTextToBlocks(asString(p.code))
        : [];

    // 若已存在 richContent 字段则直接使用，否则从旧版 blocks 自动无损转换
    const richContent =
      pType === 'note'
        ? typeof p.richContent === 'string'
          ? p.richContent
          : blocksToRichHtml(blocks)
        : undefined;

    normalizedPages.push({
      id: asString(p.id, `pg_${Date.now()}_${idx}`),
      title: asString(p.title, pType === 'note' ? `笔记_${idx + 1}` : `片段_${idx + 1}.py`),
      pageType: pType,
      language: asString(p.language, 'python'),
      code: asString(p.code),
      blocks,
      richContent
    });
  }

  if (normalizedPages.length === 0) {
    normalizedPages.push({
      id: `pg_default_${Date.now()}`,
      title: '笔记_1',
      pageType: 'note',
      language: 'python',
      code: '',
      blocks: [],
      richContent: '<p></p>'
    });
  }

  const isOwner = src.isOwner !== undefined ? Boolean(src.isOwner) : true;
  const rawShares = Array.isArray(src.shares)
    ? (src.shares as Array<Record<string, unknown>>)
        .map((s): NoteShareItem => ({
          userId: asString(s.userId || s.user_id),
          username: asString(s.username),
          name: asString(s.name),
          permission: s.permission === 'edit' ? 'edit' : 'read'
        }))
        .filter((s) => Boolean(s.userId))
    : [];
  const rawPerm = asString(src.sharePermission);
  const sharePermission: 'owner' | 'edit' | 'read' | 'public' =
    rawPerm === 'owner' || rawPerm === 'edit' || rawPerm === 'read' || rawPerm === 'public'
      ? rawPerm
      : isOwner
        ? 'owner'
        : Boolean(src.isPublic)
          ? 'public'
          : 'read';
  const canEdit =
    src.canEdit !== undefined ? Boolean(src.canEdit) : isOwner || sharePermission === 'edit';

  return {
    id: asString(src.id),
    folderId: typeof src.folderId === 'string' ? src.folderId : null,
    title: asString(src.title, '未命名笔记'),
    content: '',
    codePages: normalizedPages,
    isPublic: Boolean(src.isPublic),
    isOwner,
    canEdit,
    sharePermission,
    shares: rawShares,
    authorName: asString(src.authorName),
    updatedAt: asString(src.updatedAt),
    version: typeof src.version === 'string' ? src.version : undefined
  };
}

interface NoteStore {
  folders: NoteFolder[];
  notes: NoteItem[];
  selectedFolderId: string | null;
  activeNoteId: string | null;
  isLoading: boolean;
  serverVersion: string;

  loadNotesData: (silent?: boolean) => Promise<void>;
  checkRemoteVersion: () => Promise<boolean>;
  setSelectedFolderId: (id: string | null) => void;
  setActiveNoteId: (id: string | null) => void;
  createFolder: (name: string) => Promise<void>;
  deleteFolder: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
  saveNote: (payload: {
    id?: string | null;
    folderId: string | null;
    title: string;
    content?: string;
    codePages: CodePage[];
    isPublic?: boolean;
  }) => Promise<NoteItem | null>;
  toggleNotePublic: (noteId: string, isPublic: boolean) => Promise<boolean>;
  updateNoteShares: (
    noteId: string,
    payload: { isPublic: boolean; shares: Array<{ userId: string; permission: 'read' | 'edit' }> }
  ) => Promise<boolean>;
  deleteNote: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
  importNotesBackup: (backupData: { folders?: Array<{ name: string }>; notes?: unknown[] }) => Promise<number>;
  saveNoteFromAI: (title: string, textContent: string, codeSnippets?: string[]) => Promise<NoteItem | null>;

  // 全局未保存切换拦截守卫
  hasUnsavedChanges: boolean;
  unsavedGuardOpen: boolean;
  isSavingBeforeLeave: boolean;
  pendingNavigationAction: (() => void) | null;
  saveCurrentDraftHandler: (() => Promise<boolean>) | null;
  discardCurrentDraftHandler: (() => void) | null;

  setUnsavedState: (dirty: boolean) => void;
  registerDraftHandlers: (
    saveFn: (() => Promise<boolean>) | null,
    discardFn: (() => void) | null
  ) => void;
  requestProtectedAction: (action: () => void, autoSaveEnabled: boolean) => Promise<void>;
  resolveUnsavedGuard: (choice: 'save' | 'discard' | 'cancel') => Promise<void>;
}

export const useNoteStore = create<NoteStore>((set, get) => ({
  folders: [],
  notes: [],
  selectedFolderId: null,
  activeNoteId: null,
  isLoading: false,
  serverVersion: '',

  hasUnsavedChanges: false,
  unsavedGuardOpen: false,
  isSavingBeforeLeave: false,
  pendingNavigationAction: null,
  saveCurrentDraftHandler: null,
  discardCurrentDraftHandler: null,

  setUnsavedState: (dirty) => set({ hasUnsavedChanges: dirty }),

  registerDraftHandlers: (saveFn, discardFn) =>
    set({
      saveCurrentDraftHandler: saveFn,
      discardCurrentDraftHandler: discardFn
    }),

  requestProtectedAction: async (action, autoSaveEnabled) => {
    const state = get();
    if (!state.hasUnsavedChanges) {
      action();
      return;
    }

    // 若用户开启了“自动保存”，切页/切笔记前直接静默保存后放行
    if (autoSaveEnabled) {
      set({ isSavingBeforeLeave: true });
      try {
        const ok = state.saveCurrentDraftHandler ? await state.saveCurrentDraftHandler() : true;
        if (ok) {
          set({ hasUnsavedChanges: false });
          action();
        }
      } finally {
        set({ isSavingBeforeLeave: false });
      }
      return;
    }

    // 未开启自动保存：打开全局确认弹窗
    set({
      unsavedGuardOpen: true,
      pendingNavigationAction: action
    });
  },

  resolveUnsavedGuard: async (choice) => {
    const state = get();
    const nextAction = state.pendingNavigationAction;

    if (choice === 'cancel') {
      set({ unsavedGuardOpen: false, pendingNavigationAction: null });
      return;
    }

    if (choice === 'discard') {
      state.discardCurrentDraftHandler?.();
      set({
        hasUnsavedChanges: false,
        unsavedGuardOpen: false,
        pendingNavigationAction: null
      });
      nextAction?.();
      return;
    }

    if (choice === 'save') {
      set({ isSavingBeforeLeave: true });
      try {
        const ok = state.saveCurrentDraftHandler ? await state.saveCurrentDraftHandler() : true;
        if (ok) {
          set({
            hasUnsavedChanges: false,
            unsavedGuardOpen: false,
            pendingNavigationAction: null
          });
          nextAction?.();
        }
      } finally {
        set({ isSavingBeforeLeave: false });
      }
    }
  },

  loadNotesData: async (silent = false) => {
    if (!silent) set({ isLoading: true });
    try {
      const data = await api.notes.getAll();
      const normalized = (data.notes || []).map(normalizeNoteItem);
      set((state) => {
        const keepActive = normalized.some((n) => n.id === state.activeNoteId);
        return {
          folders: data.folders || [],
          notes: normalized,
          activeNoteId: keepActive ? state.activeNoteId : (normalized[0]?.id ?? null),
          serverVersion: data.version || state.serverVersion
        };
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('加载云端笔记失败:', err);
    } finally {
      if (!silent) set({ isLoading: false });
    }
  },

  checkRemoteVersion: async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api.notes.getVersion();
      const currentVer = get().serverVersion;
      if (currentVer && res.version && res.version !== currentVer) {
        return true;
      }
      if (!currentVer && res.version) {
        set({ serverVersion: res.version });
      }
      return false;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  },

  setSelectedFolderId: (id) => set({ selectedFolderId: id }),
  setActiveNoteId: (id) => set({ activeNoteId: id }),

  createFolder: async (name) => {
    const created = await api.notes.createFolder(name);
    set((state) => ({ folders: [...state.folders, created], selectedFolderId: created.id }));
  },

  deleteFolder: async (id) => {
    await api.notes.deleteFolder(id);
    set((state) => ({
      folders: state.folders.filter((f) => f.id !== id),
      notes: state.notes.filter((n) => n.folderId !== id),
      selectedFolderId: state.selectedFolderId === id ? null : state.selectedFolderId
    }));
  },

  saveNote: async ({ id, folderId, title, codePages, isPublic }) => {
    const finalTitle = title.trim() || formatDefaultNoteTitle();
    try {
      const res = await api.notes.save({
        id: id || undefined,
        folder_id: folderId,
        title: finalTitle,
        content: '',
        code_pages: codePages,
        is_public: isPublic
      });
      const saved = normalizeNoteItem(res);
      const nextVersion = typeof res?.version === 'string' ? res.version : '';

      set((state) => {
        const exists = state.notes.some((n) => n.id === saved.id);
        const nextNotes = exists
          ? state.notes.map((n) => (n.id === saved.id ? saved : n))
          : [saved, ...state.notes];
        return {
          notes: nextNotes,
          activeNoteId: saved.id,
          serverVersion: nextVersion || state.serverVersion
        };
      });
      return saved;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('保存笔记失败:', err);
      return null;
    }
  },

  toggleNotePublic: async (noteId, isPublic) => {
    try {
      const res = await api.notes.togglePublic(noteId, isPublic);
      set((state) => ({
        notes: state.notes.map((n) => (n.id === noteId ? { ...n, isPublic: res.isPublic } : n)),
        serverVersion: res.version || state.serverVersion
      }));
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('切换笔记公开状态失败:', err);
      return false;
    }
  },

  updateNoteShares: async (noteId, payload) => {
    try {
      const res = await api.notes.updateShares({
        note_id: noteId,
        is_public: payload.isPublic,
        shares: payload.shares.map((s) => ({ user_id: s.userId, permission: s.permission }))
      });
      set((state) => ({
        notes: state.notes.map((n) =>
          n.id === noteId
            ? {
                ...n,
                isPublic: res.isPublic,
                shares: Array.isArray(res.shares) ? res.shares : []
              }
            : n
        ),
        serverVersion: res.version || state.serverVersion
      }));
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('更新笔记共享权限失败:', err);
      return false;
    }
  },

  deleteNote: async (id) => {
    await api.notes.deleteNote(id);
    set((state) => {
      const remaining = state.notes.filter((n) => n.id !== id);
      return {
        notes: remaining,
        activeNoteId: state.activeNoteId === id ? (remaining[0]?.id ?? null) : state.activeNoteId
      };
    });
  },

  importNotesBackup: async (backupData) => {
    const state = get();
    const folderNameMap = new Map<string, string>();
    state.folders.forEach((f) => folderNameMap.set(f.name, f.id));

    if (Array.isArray(backupData.folders)) {
      for (const f of backupData.folders) {
        if (f.name && !folderNameMap.has(f.name)) {
          const created = await api.notes.createFolder(f.name);
          folderNameMap.set(created.name, created.id);
        }
      }
    }

    let importedCount = 0;
    if (Array.isArray(backupData.notes)) {
      for (const rawNote of backupData.notes) {
        const normalized = normalizeNoteItem(rawNote);
        const source = (rawNote && typeof rawNote === 'object' ? rawNote : {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
        const folderName = typeof source.folderName === 'string' ? source.folderName : '';
        let targetFolderId = state.selectedFolderId;
        if (folderName && folderNameMap.has(folderName)) {
          targetFolderId = folderNameMap.get(folderName) || null;
        }
        await api.notes.save({
          folder_id: targetFolderId,
          title: normalized.title || formatDefaultNoteTitle(),
          content: '',
          code_pages: normalized.codePages,
          is_public: Boolean(normalized.isPublic)
        });
        importedCount++;
      }
    }

    await get().loadNotesData();
    return importedCount;
  },

  saveNoteFromAI: async (title, textContent, codeSnippets = []) => {
    const pages: CodePage[] = [];
    if (textContent.trim()) {
      const blocks = parseLegacyTextToBlocks(textContent);
      pages.push({
        id: `np_ai_${Date.now()}`,
        title: 'AI说明笔记',
        pageType: 'note',
        language: 'python',
        code: '',
        blocks,
        richContent: blocksToRichHtml(blocks)
      });
    }
    codeSnippets.forEach((code, idx) => {
      pages.push({
        id: `cp_ai_${Date.now()}_${idx}`,
        title: `AI代码_${idx + 1}.py`,
        pageType: 'code',
        language: 'python',
        code,
        blocks: []
      });
    });
    return get().saveNote({
      folderId: null,
      title: title || formatDefaultNoteTitle(),
      codePages: pages
    });
  }
}));
```
