``` ts
// src/store/useFileStore.ts
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { api, ApiError } from '../utils/api';

export interface FileNode {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  size: number | null;
  isFolder: boolean;
  uploadTime: string;
  parentId: string | null;
  ownerId: string;
  isPublicFolder?: boolean;
  sharedWith?: string[];
  virtualPath?: string;
}

export interface OperationLog {
  id: string;
  time: string;
  operatorName: string;
  operatorUsername: string;
  action: '上传' | '删除' | '重命名' | '移动' | '创建文件夹' | '权限变更';
  targetName: string;
  details?: string;
}

export interface PathNode {
  id: string;
  name: string;
}

export interface UploadTask {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  size: number;
  progress: number;
  status: 'uploading' | 'success' | 'error';
  userId?: string;
}

// 剪贴板快照节点（避免跨目录切换后原节点在当前视图被卸载导致无法粘贴）
export interface ClipboardItemNode {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  virtualPath: string;
  isPublicFolder: boolean;
  isFolder: boolean;
}

export interface ClipboardState {
  items: ClipboardItemNode[];
  action: 'cut' | 'copy';
}

export interface FileStore {
  files: FileNode[];
  currentPath: PathNode[];
  tasks: UploadTask[];
  clipboard: ClipboardState | null;
  operationLogs: OperationLog[];

  enterFolder: (id: string, name: string) => void;
  goToBreadcrumb: (index: number) => void;
  deleteFile: (id: string, operator?: { name: string; username: string }) => void;
  batchDeleteFiles: (ids: string[]) => Promise<void>;
  createFolder: (
    name: string,
    parentId: string | null,
    ownerId: string,
    isPublic?: boolean,
    operator?: { name: string; username: string }
  ) => void;
  addFile: (file: FileNode, operator?: { name: string; username: string }) => void;
  renameNode: (id: string, newName: string, operator?: { name: string; username: string }) => void;
  moveNode: (
    ids: string | string[],
    targetParentId: string | null,
    operator?: { name: string; username: string }
  ) => Promise<void>;
  pasteClipboard: (targetParentId: string | null) => Promise<void>;
  updateFileShare: (ids: string | string[], sharedWithUserIds: string[]) => Promise<void>;
  loadServerFiles: (isPublic: boolean, subPath?: string) => Promise<void>;
  loadAuditLogs: () => Promise<void>;
  setClipboard: (item: ClipboardState | null) => void;
  addTask: (task: UploadTask) => void;
  updateTaskProgress: (id: string, progress: number, status?: UploadTask['status']) => void;
  removeTask: (id: string) => void;
}

// 辅助函数：根据目标父节点 ID 解析出后端所需的 target_virtual_path 与 target_is_public
function resolveTargetDestination(
  state: { files: FileNode[]; currentPath: PathNode[] },
  targetParentId: string | null
): { targetVirtualPath: string; isTargetPublic: boolean } {
  const currentPathNames = state.currentPath
    .filter((p) => p.id !== 'folder_public_root')
    .map((p) => p.name);
  const baseVirtualPath = currentPathNames.join('/');

  if (targetParentId === 'folder_public_root') {
    return { targetVirtualPath: '', isTargetPublic: true };
  }
  if (targetParentId === null) {
    // 关键修复：targetParentId 为 null 代表个人空间根目录，强制重置 isTargetPublic 为 false
    return { targetVirtualPath: '', isTargetPublic: false };
  }

  const breadcrumbIndex = state.currentPath.findIndex((p) => p.id === targetParentId);
  if (breadcrumbIndex !== -1) {
    const targetPathNames = state.currentPath
      .slice(0, breadcrumbIndex + 1)
      .filter((p) => p.id !== 'folder_public_root')
      .map((p) => p.name);
    return {
      targetVirtualPath: targetPathNames.join('/'),
      isTargetPublic: state.currentPath[0]?.id === 'folder_public_root'
    };
  }

  const targetNode = state.files.find((f) => f.id === targetParentId);
  if (targetNode) {
    const targetVirtualPath =
      targetNode.virtualPath ||
      (baseVirtualPath ? `${baseVirtualPath}/${targetNode.name}` : targetNode.name);
    return {
      targetVirtualPath,
      isTargetPublic: !!targetNode.isPublicFolder
    };
  }

  return {
    targetVirtualPath: baseVirtualPath,
    isTargetPublic: state.currentPath[0]?.id === 'folder_public_root'
  };
}

function getCurrentFolderContext(state: { files: FileNode[]; currentPath: PathNode[] }) {
  const currentFolderId =
    state.currentPath.length > 0 ? state.currentPath[state.currentPath.length - 1].id : null;
  const isInPublicSpace =
    currentFolderId === 'folder_public_root' ||
    state.currentPath[0]?.id === 'folder_public_root' ||
    !!state.files.find((f) => f.id === currentFolderId)?.isPublicFolder;
  const virtualSubPath =
    currentFolderId && currentFolderId !== 'folder_public_root'
      ? currentFolderId.replace(/^(pub_|pri_)/, '')
      : '';
  return { currentFolderId, isInPublicSpace, virtualSubPath };
}

// 当前所在目录路径的会话级持久化（刷新页面后仍停留在原文件夹）
const PATH_STORAGE_KEY = 'fc_current_path_v1';

function loadPersistedPath(): PathNode[] {
  try {
    const raw = sessionStorage.getItem(PATH_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) return parsed;
    }
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
  return [];
}

function savePersistedPath(path: PathNode[]) {
  try {
    sessionStorage.setItem(PATH_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(path));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export const useFileStore = create<FileStore>((set, get) => ({
  files: [
    {
      id: 'folder_public_root',
      name: '📂 公共共享空间',
      size: null,
      isFolder: true,
      uploadTime: '系统内置',
      parentId: null,
      ownerId: 'system',
      isPublicFolder: true
    }
  ],
  currentPath: loadPersistedPath(),
  tasks: [],
  clipboard: null,
  operationLogs: [],

  enterFolder: (id, name) =>
    set((state) => {
      const nextPath = [...state.currentPath, { id, name }];
      savePersistedPath(nextPath);
      return { currentPath: nextPath };
    }),

  goToBreadcrumb: (index) =>
    set((state) => {
      const nextPath = index === -1 ? [] : state.currentPath.slice(0, index + 1);
      savePersistedPath(nextPath);
      return { currentPath: nextPath };
    }),

  loadServerFiles: async (isPublic, subPath = '') => {
    try {
      const serverNodes = (await api.files.list(isPublic, subPath)) as unknown as FileNode[];
      set((state) => {
        const targetParentId = subPath
          ? isPublic
            ? `pub_${subPath}`
            : `pri_${subPath}`
          : isPublic
          ? 'folder_public_root'
          : null;
        const filtered = state.files.filter(
          (f) => f.id === 'folder_public_root' || f.parentId !== targetParentId
        );
        return { files: [...filtered, ...serverNodes] };
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('同步服务器文件失败', err);
    }
  },

  loadAuditLogs: async () => {
    try {
      const logs = await api.audit.getLogs();
      const typedLogs = logs as unknown as Array<{
        id: string; timestamp: string; operator_name: string;
        operator_username: string; action: OperationLog['action'];
        target_name: string; details?: string;
      }>;
      const mapped = typedLogs.map((l) => ({
        id: l.id,
        time: l.timestamp,
        operatorName: l.operator_name,
        operatorUsername: l.operator_username,
        action: l.action,
        targetName: l.target_name,
        details: l.details
      }));
      set({ operationLogs: mapped });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('获取审计日志失败', err);
    }
  },

  createFolder: (name, parentId, _ownerId, isPublic) => {
    const virtualParent =
      parentId && parentId !== 'folder_public_root'
        ? parentId.replace(/^(pub_|pri_)/, '')
        : '';
    api.files.createFolder(name, virtualParent, !!isPublic).then(() => {
      get().loadServerFiles(!!isPublic, virtualParent);
    });
  },

  addFile: (file) => {
    set((state) => ({ files: [...state.files, file] }));
  },

  deleteFile: (id) => {
    const target = get().files.find((f) => f.id === id);
    if (!target || target.id === 'folder_public_root') return;

    const virtualPath = target.virtualPath || target.name;
    const isPublic = !!target.isPublicFolder;

    api.files.delete(virtualPath, isPublic).then(() => {
      set((state) => ({ files: state.files.filter((f) => f.id !== id) }));
    });
  },

  batchDeleteFiles: async (ids) => {
    const state = get();
    const targets = state.files.filter(
      (f) => ids.includes(f.id) && f.id !== 'folder_public_root'
    );
    if (targets.length === 0) return;

    const isPublic = !!targets[0].isPublicFolder;
    const virtualPaths = targets.map((f) => f.virtualPath || f.name);

    try {
      await api.files.batchDelete(virtualPaths, isPublic);
      const idSet = new Set(ids);
      set((s) => ({
        files: s.files.filter((f) => !idSet.has(f.id))
      }));
      if (isPublic) state.loadAuditLogs();
    } catch (err) {
      alert(`批量删除失败: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : '未知错误'}`);
    }
  },

  renameNode: (id, newName) => {
    const target = get().files.find((f) => f.id === id);
    if (!target) return;

    const virtualPath = target.virtualPath || target.name;
    const isPublic = !!target.isPublicFolder;

    api.files
      .rename(virtualPath, newName, isPublic)
      .then(() => {
        const { isInPublicSpace, virtualSubPath } = getCurrentFolderContext(get());
        get().loadServerFiles(isInPublicSpace, virtualSubPath);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        alert(`重命名失败: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : '未知错误'}`);
      });
  },

  // 支持单个或批量拖拽移动（含从公共空间拖回根目录）
  moveNode: async (ids, targetParentId) => {
    const state = get();
    const idList = Array.isArray(ids) ? ids : [ids];
    const sourceNodes = state.files.filter(
      (f) => idList.includes(f.id) && f.id !== 'folder_public_root' && f.id !== targetParentId
    );
    if (sourceNodes.length === 0) return;

    const currentPathNames = state.currentPath
      .filter((p) => p.id !== 'folder_public_root')
      .map((p) => p.name);
    const baseVirtualPath = currentPathNames.join('/');

    const sourceVirtualPaths = sourceNodes.map(
      (n) => n.virtualPath || (baseVirtualPath ? `${baseVirtualPath}/${n.name}` : n.name)
    );
    const sourceIsPublic = !!sourceNodes[0].isPublicFolder;

    const { targetVirtualPath, isTargetPublic } = resolveTargetDestination(
      state,
      targetParentId
    );

    const movedIdSet = new Set(sourceNodes.map((n) => n.id));
    set((s) => ({
      files: s.files.map((f) =>
        movedIdSet.has(f.id)
          ? { ...f, parentId: targetParentId, isPublicFolder: isTargetPublic }
          : f
      )
    }));

    const { isInPublicSpace, virtualSubPath } = getCurrentFolderContext(state);

    try {
      await api.files.move({
        source_virtual_paths: sourceVirtualPaths,
        target_virtual_path: targetVirtualPath,
        source_is_public: sourceIsPublic,
        target_is_public: isTargetPublic
      });
      await get().loadServerFiles(isInPublicSpace, virtualSubPath);
      if (sourceIsPublic || isTargetPublic) get().loadAuditLogs();
    } catch (err) {
      await get().loadServerFiles(isInPublicSpace, virtualSubPath);
      const detail =
        err instanceof ApiError
          ? err.detail || err.message
          : err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : String(err);
      alert(`移动失败: ${detail}`);
    }
  },

  // 粘贴剪贴板中的项目（自动识别剪切或复制，复制支持同目录自动生成 (1) 副本）
  pasteClipboard: async (targetParentId) => {
    const state = get();
    const clip = state.clipboard;
    if (!clip || clip.items.length === 0) return;

    const { targetVirtualPath, isTargetPublic } = resolveTargetDestination(
      state,
      targetParentId
    );
    const sourceIsPublic = clip.items[0].isPublicFolder;
    const sourceVirtualPaths = clip.items.map((item) => item.virtualPath);
    const { isInPublicSpace, virtualSubPath } = getCurrentFolderContext(state);

    try {
      if (clip.action === 'cut') {
        await api.files.move({
          source_virtual_paths: sourceVirtualPaths,
          target_virtual_path: targetVirtualPath,
          source_is_public: sourceIsPublic,
          target_is_public: isTargetPublic
        });
      } else {
        await api.files.copy({
          source_virtual_paths: sourceVirtualPaths,
          target_virtual_path: targetVirtualPath,
          source_is_public: sourceIsPublic,
          target_is_public: isTargetPublic
        });
      }
      set({ clipboard: null });
      await get().loadServerFiles(isInPublicSpace, virtualSubPath);
      if (sourceIsPublic || isTargetPublic) get().loadAuditLogs();
    } catch (err) {
      const detail =
        err instanceof ApiError
          ? err.detail || err.message
          : err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : String(err);
      alert(`${clip.action === 'cut' ? '剪切移动' : '复制'}失败: ${detail}`);
    }
  },

  updateFileShare: async (ids, sharedWithUserIds) => {
    const state = get();
    const targetIds = Array.isArray(ids) ? ids : [ids];
    const targetFiles = state.files.filter((f) => targetIds.includes(f.id));
    if (targetFiles.length === 0) return;

    const virtualPaths = targetFiles.map((f) => f.virtualPath || f.name);

    try {
      await api.files.setShare({
        virtual_paths: virtualPaths,
        shared_user_ids: sharedWithUserIds
      });

      const idSet = new Set(targetIds);
      set((s) => ({
        files: s.files.map((f) =>
          idSet.has(f.id) ? { ...f, sharedWith: sharedWithUserIds } : f
        )
      }));

      state.loadAuditLogs();
    } catch (err) {
      const detail = err instanceof ApiError ? err.detail : undefined;
      alert(`更新共享权限失败: ${detail || (err instanceof Error ? err.message : '未知错误')}`);
    }
  },

  setClipboard: (item) => set({ clipboard: item }),
  addTask: (task) => set((state) => ({ tasks: [task, ...state.tasks] })),
  updateTaskProgress: (id, progress, status) =>
    set((state) => ({
      tasks: state.tasks.map((t) =>
        t.id === id ? { ...t, progress, status: status || t.status } : t
      )
    })),
  removeTask: (id) =>
    set((state) => ({ tasks: state.tasks.filter((t) => t.id !== id) }))
}));
```
### <<< FILE END: src/store/useFileStore.ts >>> ###

### >>> FILE START [07/12]: src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx <<< ###
``` tsx
// src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx
import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { NavLink, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  FolderKanban,
  Bot,
  NotebookPen,
  MonitorPlay,
  Users,
  KeyRound,
  LogOut,
  Check
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  useAuthStore,
  LOGO_ICON_COMPONENTS,
  getThemeLogo,
  type CustomThemeItem,
  type LogoIcon,
} from '../../store/useAuthStore';
import { useNoteStore } from '../../store/useNoteStore';
import AvatarModal from '../AvatarModal';

export default function Sidebar() {
  const { currentUser, theme, themes, fetchThemes, noteAutoSave, setTheme, logout } = useAuthStore();
  const { hasUnsavedChanges, requestProtectedAction } = useNoteStore();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();

  const [isThemeMenuOpen, setIsThemeMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [avatarModalOpen, setAvatarModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const menuContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (currentUser) fetchThemes();
  }, [currentUser, fetchThemes]);

  const canUseAi = Boolean(currentUser && currentUser.role !== 'guest');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isThemeMenuOpen) return;
    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuContainerRef.current && !menuContainerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setIsThemeMenuOpen(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, [isThemeMenuOpen]);

  const handleGuardedNav = (e: React.MouseEvent, targetPath: string) => {
    if (location.pathname.startsWith(targetPath)) return;
    if (hasUnsavedChanges) {
      e.preventDefault();
      requestProtectedAction(() => navigate(targetPath), noteAutoSave);
    }
  };

  const handleGuardedLogout = () => {
    if (hasUnsavedChanges) {
      requestProtectedAction(() => logout(), noteAutoSave);
    } else {
      logout();
    }
  };

  const renderThemeLogo = (item: CustomThemeItem | undefined, size = 16) => {
    const icon: LogoIcon = getThemeLogo(item).icon;
    if (icon === 'ring') {
      const side = Math.max(10, size - 1);
      return (
        <span
          className="rounded-full inline-block shrink-0"
          style={{
            width: `${side}px`,
            height: `${side}px`,
            border: '2px solid currentColor',
            backgroundColor: 'transparent',
          }}
        />
      );
    }
    const Icon = LOGO_ICON_COMPONENTS[icon] || LOGO_ICON_COMPONENTS.sparkle;
    return <Icon size={size} />;
  };

  const activeTheme = themes.find((item) => item.id === theme);
  const visibleThemes = themes.filter((item) => item.activeInMenu !== false);

  return (
    <div ref={menuContainerRef} className="contents">
      {/* 桌面端左侧纵向导航栏 */}
      <aside className="hidden md:flex w-16 bg-[#16181d] border-r border-gray-800 flex-col items-center py-5 select-none shrink-0 z-30 relative">
        <div
          style={{
            backgroundColor: getThemeLogo(activeTheme).bg,
            color: getThemeLogo(activeTheme).fg,
          }}
          className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center font-black text-base shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/20 mb-8"
        >
          {renderThemeLogo(activeTheme, 20)}
        </div>

        <nav className="flex flex-col gap-4 flex-1 w-full px-2">
          <NavLink
            to="/file-center"
            onClick={(e) => handleGuardedNav(e, '/file-center')}
            className={({ isActive }) =>
              `flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-3 rounded-xl transition-all ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-blue-600/15 text-blue-400 font-medium'
                  : 'text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-800/60 hover:text-gray-200'
              }`
            }
            title="文件管理中心"
          >
            <FolderKanban size={20} className="mb-1" />
            <span className="text-[10px]">文件</span>
          </NavLink>

          {canUseAi && (
            <NavLink
              to="/ai-copilot"
              onClick={(e) => handleGuardedNav(e, '/ai-copilot')}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-3 rounded-xl transition-all ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-blue-600/15 text-blue-400 font-medium'
                    : 'text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-800/60 hover:text-gray-200'
                }`
              }
              title="DCM-助手"
            >
              <Bot size={20} className="mb-1" />
              <span className="text-[10px]">助手</span>
            </NavLink>
          )}

          <NavLink
            to="/notes"
            onClick={(e) => handleGuardedNav(e, '/notes')}
            className={({ isActive }) =>
              `flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-3 rounded-xl transition-all relative ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-blue-600/15 text-blue-400 font-medium'
                  : 'text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-800/60 hover:text-gray-200'
              }`
            }
            title="在线笔记"
          >
            <NotebookPen size={20} className="mb-1" />
            <span className="text-[10px]">笔记</span>
            {hasUnsavedChanges && (
              <span
                className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-amber-400 absolute top-2 right-2.5 animate-pulse"
                title="有未保存的笔记修改"
              />
            )}
          </NavLink>

          <NavLink
            to="/screen-share"
            onClick={(e) => handleGuardedNav(e, '/screen-share')}
            className={({ isActive }) =>
              `flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-3 rounded-xl transition-all ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-blue-600/15 text-blue-400 font-medium'
                  : 'text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-800/60 hover:text-gray-200'
              }`
            }
            title="远程屏幕共享"
          >
            <MonitorPlay size={20} className="mb-1" />
            <span className="text-[10px]">投屏</span>
          </NavLink>

          {currentUser?.role === 'admin' && (
            <NavLink
              to="/users"
              onClick={(e) => handleGuardedNav(e, '/users')}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-3 rounded-xl transition-all ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-blue-600/15 text-blue-400 font-medium'
                    : 'text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-800/60 hover:text-gray-200'
                }`
              }
              title="成员与权限管理"
            >
              <Users size={20} className="mb-1" />
              <span className="text-[10px]">管理</span>
            </NavLink>
          )}
        </nav>

        {/* 底部主题与账号操作区 */}
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 mt-auto pt-4 border-t border-gray-800/80 w-full px-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsThemeMenuOpen((prev) => !prev)}
            style={{ backgroundColor: "var(--bg-hover)", borderColor: "var(--border-main)" }} className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl border hover:opacity-85 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
            title="界面主题"
          >
            {renderThemeLogo(activeTheme, 17)}
          </button>

          {currentUser?.role !== 'guest' && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => window.dispatchEvent(new Event('open_change_pwd_modal'))}
              className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-800/70 hover:text-gray-200 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
              title="修改密码"
            >
              <KeyRound size={17} />
            </button>
          )}

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleGuardedLogout}
            className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl text-gray-400 hover:bg-rose-500/15 hover:text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
            title={currentUser?.role === 'guest' ? '退出访客模式' : '退出登录'}
          >
            <LogOut size={17} />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={!currentUser || currentUser.role === 'guest'}
            onClick={() => {
              if (currentUser && currentUser.role !== 'guest') {
                setAvatarModalOpen(true);
              }
            }}
            title={currentUser && currentUser.role !== 'guest' ? '点击设置个人头像' : undefined}
            className="relative w-8 h-8 rounded-lg overflow-hidden bg-blue-600/20 border border-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold text-blue-300 shrink-0 disabled:cursor-default"
          >
            <span className="pointer-events-none select-none">
              {(currentUser?.name || currentUser?.username || '访').slice(0, 1)}
            </span>
            {currentUser?.avatarUrl && (
              <img
                src={currentUser.avatarUrl}
                alt={currentUser.name || currentUser.username || '头像'}
                className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover"
                onError={(e) => {
                  (e.currentTarget as HTMLImageElement).style.display = 'none';
                }}
              />
            )}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* 主题选择弹出面板 */}
        {isThemeMenuOpen && (
          <div
            style={{
              backgroundColor: 'var(--bg-surface)',
              borderColor: 'var(--border-main)',
              color: 'var(--text-primary)',
            }}
            className="absolute left-16 bottom-16 w-56 border rounded-xl shadow-2xl p-2 z-50"
          >
            <div
              style={{ borderColor: 'var(--border-subtle)', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold border-b mb-1"
            >
              界面外观主题
            </div>
            {visibleThemes.map((item) => {
              const active = theme === item.id;
              return (
                <button
                  key={item.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    void setTheme(item.id);
                    setIsThemeMenuOpen(false);
                  }}
                  style={
                    active
                      ? { backgroundColor: 'var(--bg-hover)', color: 'var(--text-primary)' }
                      : { color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }
                  }
                  className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-2.5 py-2 rounded-lg text-left transition-colors hover:opacity-90"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                    <span
                      className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"
                      style={{
                        backgroundColor: getThemeLogo(item).bg,
                        color: getThemeLogo(item).fg,
                      }}
                    >
                      {renderThemeLogo(item, 13)}
                    </span>
                    <div className="truncate">
                      <div className="text-xs font-medium truncate">{item.name}</div>
                      <div style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }} className="text-[10px] truncate">
                        {item.description || (item.isBuiltin ? '内置主题方案' : '自定义全局风格')}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  {active && <Check size={14} className="text-blue-500 shrink-0" />}
                </button>
              );
            })}
            {visibleThemes.length === 0 && (
              <div className="px-2.5 py-3 text-[11px] text-gray-400">暂无已激活的主题方案</div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </aside>

      {/* 移动端底部导航栏 */}
      <nav className="md:hidden fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 h-14 bg-[#16181d] border-t border-gray-800 flex items-center justify-around px-2 z-30">
        <NavLink
          to="/file-center"
          onClick={(e) => handleGuardedNav(e, '/file-center')}
          className={({ isActive }) =>
            `flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] ${
              isActive ? 'text-blue-400 font-medium' : 'text-gray-400'
            }`
          }
        >
          <FolderKanban size={18} />
          <span>文件</span>
        </NavLink>

        {canUseAi && (
          <NavLink
            to="/ai-copilot"
            onClick={(e) => handleGuardedNav(e, '/ai-copilot')}
            className={({ isActive }) =>
              `flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] ${
                isActive ? 'text-blue-400 font-medium' : 'text-gray-400'
              }`
            }
          >
            <Bot size={18} />
            <span>助手</span>
          </NavLink>
        )}

        <NavLink
          to="/notes"
          onClick={(e) => handleGuardedNav(e, '/notes')}
          className={({ isActive }) =>
            `flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] ${
              isActive ? 'text-blue-400 font-medium' : 'text-gray-400'
            }`
          }
        >
          <NotebookPen size={18} />
          <span>笔记</span>
        </NavLink>

        <NavLink
          to="/screen-share"
          onClick={(e) => handleGuardedNav(e, '/screen-share')}
          className={({ isActive }) =>
            `flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] ${
              isActive ? 'text-blue-400 font-medium' : 'text-gray-400'
            }`
          }
        >
          <MonitorPlay size={18} />
          <span>投屏</span>
        </NavLink>
      </nav>

      <AvatarModal open={avatarModalOpen} onClose={() => setAvatarModalOpen(false)} />
    </div>
  );
}
```
### <<< FILE END: src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx >>> ###

### >>> FILE START [08/12]: src/components/notes/RichTextNoteEditor.tsx <<< ###
``` tsx
﻿// src/components/notes/RichTextNoteEditor.tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useEditor, EditorContent } from '@tiptap/react';
import StarterKit from '@tiptap/starter-kit';
import { Placeholder } from '@tiptap/extensions';
import { TaskList } from '@tiptap/extension-list';
import { TableKit } from '@tiptap/extension-table';
import { TextStyle, Color } from '@tiptap/extension-text-style';
import Highlight from '@tiptap/extension-highlight';
import TextAlign from '@tiptap/extension-text-align';
import {
  Undo2,
  Redo2,
  Bold,
  Italic,
  Underline as UnderlineIcon,
  Strikethrough,
  Code,
  Heading1,
  Heading2,
  Heading3,
  List,
  ListOrdered,
  CheckSquare,
  IndentIncrease,
  IndentDecrease,
  Quote,
  Minus,
  AlignLeft,
  AlignCenter,
  AlignRight,
  Link as LinkIcon,
  Table as TableIcon,
  RemoveFormatting,
  Highlighter,
  Palette,
  Image as ImageIcon,
  Code2
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  PriorityTaskItem,
  CollapsibleCodeBlock,
  CollapsibleImageBlock
} from './RichNoteExtensions';
import { api } from '../../utils/api';

const TEXT_COLORS = [
  { label: '默认', value: '' },
  { label: '红色', value: '#ef4444' },
  { label: '橙色', value: '#f97316' },
  { label: '琥珀', value: '#eab308' },
  { label: '绿色', value: '#22c55e' },
  { label: '蓝色', value: '#3b82f6' },
  { label: '紫色', value: '#a855f7' },
  { label: '灰色', value: '#9ca3af' }
];

const HIGHLIGHT_COLORS = [
  { label: '无高亮', value: '' },
  { label: '黄高亮', value: 'rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.35)' },
  { label: '绿高亮', value: 'rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.30)' },
  { label: '蓝高亮', value: 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.30)' },
  { label: '红高亮', value: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.30)' },
  { label: '紫高亮', value: 'rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.30)' }
];

export interface RichTextNoteEditorProps {
  content: string;
  editable: boolean;
  onChange: (html: string) => void;
  onNotify?: (msg: string) => void;
}

export default function RichTextNoteEditor({
  content,
  editable,
  onChange,
  onNotify
}: RichTextNoteEditorProps) {
  const imageInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const lastEmittedRef = useRef<string>(content);
  const pasteSeqRef = useRef(0);
  const [isUploadingImg, setIsUploadingImg] = useState(false);
  const [openPicker, setOpenPicker] = useState<'color' | 'highlight' | null>(null);

  const uploadAndInsertImage = async (file: File | Blob) => {
    if (!editor || !editable) return;
    setIsUploadingImg(true);
    try {
      pasteSeqRef.current += 1;
      const fallbackName = `paste_${pasteSeqRef.current}.png`;
      const res = await api.notes.uploadImage(
        file,
        file instanceof File ? file.name : fallbackName
      );
      editor
        .chain()
        .focus()
        .insertContent({
          type: 'collapsibleImageBlock',
          attrs: {
            src: res.url,
            title: file instanceof File ? file.name : 'image.png',
            width: 75,
            collapsed: false
          }
        })
        .run();
      onNotify?.('🖼️ 图片已插入，记得点击保存笔记');
    } catch (err) {
      alert(`图片上传失败: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : '未知错误'}`);
    } finally {
      setIsUploadingImg(false);
    }
  };

  const editor = useEditor({
    extensions: [
      StarterKit.configure({
        codeBlock: false,
        link: {
          openOnClick: false,
          HTMLAttributes: {
            class: 'text-blue-400 underline cursor-pointer'
          }
        }
      }),
      Placeholder.configure({
        placeholder:
          '在此输入笔记内容... 支持完整富文本排版、直接按 Ctrl+V 粘贴图片、点击上方按钮插入待办事项或折叠代码块。'
      }),
      TextStyle,
      Color,
      Highlight.configure({ multicolor: true }),
      TextAlign.configure({ types: ['heading', 'paragraph'] }),
      TaskList,
      PriorityTaskItem.configure({ nested: true }),
      TableKit.configure({
        table: { resizable: false }
      }),
      CollapsibleCodeBlock,
      CollapsibleImageBlock
    ],
    content: content || '',
    editable,
    shouldRerenderOnTransaction: true,
    onUpdate: ({ editor: ed }) => {
      const html = ed.getHTML();
      lastEmittedRef.current = html;
      onChange(html);
    },
    editorProps: {
      attributes: {
        class: 'tiptap-editor focus:outline-none min-h-[420px] select-text'
      },
      handlePaste: (_view, event) => {
        if (!editable) return false;
        const items = event.clipboardData?.items;
        if (items) {
          for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
            const item = items[i];
            if (item.type.startsWith('image/')) {
              const blob = item.getAsFile();
              if (blob) {
                event.preventDefault();
                uploadAndInsertImage(blob);
                return true;
              }
            }
          }
        }

        const text = event.clipboardData?.getData('text/plain') || '';
        if (text.startsWith('__NOTE_IMG__:')) {
          const imgUrl = text.replace('__NOTE_IMG__:', '').trim();
          if (imgUrl && editor) {
            event.preventDefault();
            editor
              .chain()
              .focus()
              .insertContent({
                type: 'collapsibleImageBlock',
                attrs: {
                  src: imgUrl,
                  title: 'image.png',
                  width: 75,
                  collapsed: false
                }
              })
              .run();
            return true;
          }
        }
        return false;
      }
    }
  });

  // 同步外部只读状态变化
  useEffect(() => {
    if (editor && editor.isEditable !== editable) {
      editor.setEditable(editable);
    }
  }, [editor, editable]);

  // 切换笔记/页签或远端自动同步时更新画布内容（不干扰本地连续输入光标）
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!editor) return;
    if (content !== lastEmittedRef.current && content !== editor.getHTML()) {
      lastEmittedRef.current = content;
      editor.commands.setContent(content || '', { emitUpdate: false });
    }
  }, [editor, content]);

  if (!editor) return null;

  const isInTaskItem = editor.isActive('taskItem');
  const isInTable = editor.isActive('table');

  const handleSetLink = () => {
    const previousUrl = editor.getAttributes('link').href || '';
    const url = window.prompt('请输入链接地址 (留空则移除链接):', previousUrl);
    if (url === null) return;
    if (url.trim() === '') {
      editor.chain().focus().extendMarkRange('link').unsetLink().run();
      return;
    }
    const normalizedUrl = /^https?:\/\//i.test(url.trim()) ? url.trim() : `https://${url.trim()}`;
    editor.chain().focus().extendMarkRange('link').setLink({ href: normalizedUrl }).run();
  };

  const handleInsertCodeWindow = () => {
    editor
      .chain()
      .focus()
      .insertContent({
        type: 'collapsibleCodeBlock',
        attrs: {
          code: '',
          language: 'python',
          title: '代码片段.py',
          collapsed: false
        }
      })
      .run();
  };

  const toolBtn = (active: boolean) =>
    `p-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs transition-colors flex items-center justify-center ${
      active
        ? 'bg-blue-600/25 text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/50'
        : 'text-gray-300 hover:bg-[#323640] hover:text-white border border-transparent'
    }`;

  return (
    <div
      onClick={() => setOpenPicker(null)}
      className="max-w-5xl mx-auto min-h-full bg-[#1b1e26] border border-gray-800/80 rounded-2xl shadow-inner flex flex-col overflow-visible"
    >
      <input
        type="file"
        ref={imageInputRef}
        accept="image/*"
        className="hidden"
        onChange={(e) => {
          const file = e.target.files?.[0];
          if (file) uploadAndInsertImage(file);
          e.target.value = '';
        }}
      />

      {/* 完整富文本编辑器工具栏（访客/只读模式下自动隐藏） */}
      {editable && (
        <div className="rich-editor-toolbar sticky top-0 z-20 px-3 py-2 border-b border-gray-800 rounded-t-2xl flex items-center flex-wrap gap-1 select-none">
          {/* 撤销 / 重做 */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().undo().run()}
            disabled={!editor.can().undo()}
            className={`${toolBtn(false)} disabled:opacity-35`}
            title="撤销 (Ctrl+Z)"
          >
            <Undo2 size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().redo().run()}
            disabled={!editor.can().redo()}
            className={`${toolBtn(false)} disabled:opacity-35`}
            title="重做 (Ctrl+Y)"
          >
            <Redo2 size={14} />
          </button>

          <span className="w-px h-4 bg-gray-700 mx-1" />

          {/* 标题层级 H1 ~ H3 */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: 1 }).run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive('heading', { level: 1 }))}
            title="一级标题 (H1)"
          >
            <Heading1 size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: 2 }).run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive('heading', { level: 2 }))}
            title="二级标题 (H2)"
          >
            <Heading2 size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: 3 }).run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive('heading', { level: 3 }))}
            title="三级标题 (H3)"
          >
            <Heading3 size={14} />
          </button>

          <span className="w-px h-4 bg-gray-700 mx-1" />

          {/* 字符样式 */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBold().run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive('bold'))}
            title="加粗 (Ctrl+B)"
          >
            <Bold size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleItalic().run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive('italic'))}
            title="斜体 (Ctrl+I)"
          >
            <Italic size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleUnderline().run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive('underline'))}
            title="下划线 (Ctrl+U)"
          >
            <UnderlineIcon size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleStrike().run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive('strike'))}
            title="删除线"
          >
            <Strikethrough size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleCode().run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive('code'))}
            title="行内代码"
          >
            <Code size={14} />
          </button>

          {/* 文字颜色选择器 */}
          <div className="relative" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setOpenPicker((prev) => (prev === 'color' ? null : 'color'))}
              className={toolBtn(openPicker === 'color')}
              title="文字颜色"
            >
              <Palette size={14} />
            </button>
            {openPicker === 'color' && (
              <div className="absolute left-0 top-full mt-1.5 p-2 bg-[#252830] border border-gray-700 rounded-xl shadow-2xl flex items-center gap-1.5 z-50">
                {TEXT_COLORS.map((c) => (
                  <button
                    key={c.label}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      if (c.value) {
                        editor.chain().focus().setColor(c.value).run();
                      } else {
                        editor.chain().focus().unsetColor().run();
                      }
                      setOpenPicker(null);
                    }}
                    className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full border border-gray-600 flex items-center justify-center text-[9px] hover:scale-110 transition-transform"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: c.value || 'transparent' }}
                    title={c.label}
                  >
                    {!c.value && '×'}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* 荧光高亮选择器 */}
          <div className="relative" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setOpenPicker((prev) => (prev === 'highlight' ? null : 'highlight'))}
              className={toolBtn(editor.isActive('highlight') || openPicker === 'highlight')}
              title="文字背景高亮"
            >
              <Highlighter size={14} />
            </button>
            {openPicker === 'highlight' && (
              <div className="absolute left-0 top-full mt-1.5 p-2 bg-[#252830] border border-gray-700 rounded-xl shadow-2xl flex items-center gap-1.5 z-50">
                {HIGHLIGHT_COLORS.map((c) => (
                  <button
                    key={c.label}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      if (c.value) {
                        editor.chain().focus().toggleHighlight({ color: c.value }).run();
                      } else {
                        editor.chain().focus().unsetHighlight().run();
                      }
                      setOpenPicker(null);
                    }}
                    className="w-5 h-5 rounded-md border border-gray-600 flex items-center justify-center text-[9px] hover:scale-110 transition-transform"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: c.value || 'transparent' }}
                    title={c.label}
                  >
                    {!c.value && '×'}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <span className="w-px h-4 bg-gray-700 mx-1" />

          {/* 对齐方式 */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('left').run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'left' }))}
            title="左对齐"
          >
            <AlignLeft size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('center').run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'center' }))}
            title="居中对齐"
          >
            <AlignCenter size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('right').run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'right' }))}
            title="右对齐"
          >
            <AlignRight size={14} />
          </button>

          <span className="w-px h-4 bg-gray-700 mx-1" />

          {/* 列表与核心待办按钮 */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBulletList().run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive('bulletList'))}
            title="无序列表"
          >
            <List size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleOrderedList().run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive('orderedList'))}
            title="有序列表"
          >
            <ListOrdered size={14} />
          </button>

          {/* 插入待办事项核心按钮 */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleTaskList().run()}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs border transition-colors ${
              editor.isActive('taskList')
                ? 'bg-emerald-600/25 border-emerald-500/60 text-emerald-300 font-medium'
                : 'bg-emerald-600/15 hover:bg-emerald-600/25 border-emerald-500/35 text-emerald-300'
            }`}
            title="在光标处插入带重要等级的待办事项（支持子待办与点击勾选划掉）"
          >
            <CheckSquare size={13} />
            <span>待办</span>
          </button>

          {/* 当光标位于待办中时，提供直观的升降级按钮（对应 Tab / Shift+Tab） */}
          {isInTaskItem && (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().sinkListItem('taskItem').run()}
                disabled={!editor.can().sinkListItem('taskItem')}
                className={`${toolBtn(false)} disabled:opacity-35`}
                title="降级为子待办 (Tab)"
              >
                <IndentIncrease size={14} />
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().liftListItem('taskItem').run()}
                disabled={!editor.can().liftListItem('taskItem')}
                className={`${toolBtn(false)} disabled:opacity-35`}
                title="升级为上级待办 (Shift+Tab)"
              >
                <IndentDecrease size={14} />
              </button>
            </>
          )}

          <span className="w-px h-4 bg-gray-700 mx-1" />

          {/* 引用 / 分割线 / 链接 / 表格 / 清除格式 */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBlockquote().run()}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive('blockquote'))}
            title="引用块"
          >
            <Quote size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setHorizontalRule().run()}
            className={toolBtn(false)}
            title="插入分割线"
          >
            <Minus size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleSetLink}
            className={toolBtn(editor.isActive('link'))}
            title="插入或编辑超链接"
          >
            <LinkIcon size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() =>
              editor.chain().focus().insertTable({ rows: 3, cols: 3, withHeaderRow: true }).run()
            }
            className={toolBtn(isInTable)}
            title="插入 3×3 表格"
          >
            <TableIcon size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().unsetAllMarks().clearNodes().run()}
            className={toolBtn(false)}
            title="一键清除选中文本格式"
          >
            <RemoveFormatting size={14} />
          </button>

          {/* 表格上下文快捷操作 */}
          {isInTable && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 ml-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-[#16181d] border border-gray-700 text-[11px]">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().addRowAfter().run()}
                className="text-blue-400 hover:underline px-1"
              >
                +行
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().addColumnAfter().run()}
                className="text-blue-400 hover:underline px-1"
              >
                +列
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().deleteRow().run()}
                className="text-yellow-400 hover:underline px-1"
              >
                -行
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().deleteColumn().run()}
                className="text-yellow-400 hover:underline px-1"
              >
                -列
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().deleteTable().run()}
                className="text-red-400 hover:underline px-1"
              >
                删表
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          <span className="w-px h-4 bg-gray-700 mx-1" />

          {/* 插入图片与折叠代码窗 */}
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={isUploadingImg}
            onClick={() => imageInputRef.current?.click()}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-emerald-600/20 hover:bg-emerald-600/30 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/40 rounded-lg transition-colors"
            title="上传图片插入到当前光标处（支持 Ctrl+V 粘贴截图）"
          >
            <ImageIcon size={13} />
            <span>{isUploadingImg ? '上传中...' : '图片'}</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleInsertCodeWindow}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded-lg transition-colors shadow"
            title="在当前光标处插入可展开/收起的高亮代码块窗口"
          >
            <Code2 size={13} />
            <span>代码块</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 主画布编辑区域 */}
      <div className="flex-1 px-5 md:px-10 py-6">
        <EditorContent editor={editor} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### >>> FILE START [09/12]: src/components/notes/RichNoteExtensions.tsx <<< ###
``` tsx
/* eslint-disable react-refresh/only-export-components */
// src/components/notes/RichNoteExtensions.tsx
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Node, mergeAttributes } from '@tiptap/core';
import { TaskItem } from '@tiptap/extension-list';
import {
  ReactNodeViewRenderer,
  NodeViewWrapper,
  NodeViewContent
} from '@tiptap/react';
import type { NodeViewProps } from '@tiptap/react';
import {
  Check,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Copy,
  Image as ImageIcon,
  ListPlus,
  Scissors,
  Trash2,
  WrapText
} from 'lucide-react';
import { SyntaxHighlightEditor } from './SyntaxHighlightEditor';

// ============================================================================
// 1. 4 级重要度多级待办事项节点 (PriorityTaskItem)
//    - 4 级重要度：紧急 (high) / 重要 (medium) / 普通 (normal) / 低 (low)
//    - 父子状态独立：勾选父项仅划掉父项文本，不强制联动子项
//    - 父项自动显示子待办完成进度（例如 1/3）
//    - 支持行内 "+ 子待办" 按钮与键盘 Tab / Shift+Tab 升降级
// ============================================================================
export type TodoPriority = 'high' | 'medium' | 'normal' | 'low';

export const TODO_PRIORITY_LIST: Array<{
  key: TodoPriority;
  label: string;
  dotColor: string;
}> = [
  { key: 'high', label: '紧急', dotColor: '#ef4444' },
  { key: 'medium', label: '重要', dotColor: '#f59e0b' },
  { key: 'normal', label: '普通', dotColor: '#3b82f6' },
  { key: 'low', label: '低', dotColor: '#9ca3af' }
];

const PRIORITY_LABEL_MAP: Record<TodoPriority, string> = {
  high: '紧急',
  medium: '重要',
  normal: '普通',
  low: '低'
};

function PriorityTaskItemView({
  node,
  updateAttributes,
  editor,
  getPos
}: NodeViewProps) {
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const checked = Boolean(node.attrs.checked);
  const priority: TodoPriority =
    (['high', 'medium', 'normal', 'low'].includes(node.attrs.priority)
      ? node.attrs.priority
      : 'normal') as TodoPriority;

  // 统计当前待办下所有嵌套子待办的完成进度 (completed / total)
  let subTotal = 0;
  let subCompleted = 0;
  node.forEach((child) => {
    if (child.type.name === 'taskList') {
      child.descendants((desc) => {
        if (desc.type.name === 'taskItem') {
          subTotal += 1;
          if (desc.attrs.checked) subCompleted += 1;
        }
      });
    }
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!menuOpen) return;
    const onOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(e.target as globalThis.Node)) {
        setMenuOpen(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onOutside);
  }, [menuOpen]);

  const handleToggleChecked = () => {
    if (!editor.isEditable) return;
    updateAttributes({ checked: !checked });
  };

  const handleSelectPriority = (nextPrio: TodoPriority) => {
    if (!editor.isEditable) return;
    updateAttributes({ priority: nextPrio });
    setMenuOpen(false);
  };

  // 在当前待办项内部插入一条子待办并自动聚焦
  const handleAddSubTask = () => {
    if (!editor.isEditable) return;
    const pos = typeof getPos === 'function' ? getPos() : undefined;
    if (typeof pos !== 'number') return;

    const hasSubList = node.lastChild?.type.name === 'taskList';
    const insertPos = hasSubList ? pos + node.nodeSize - 2 : pos + node.nodeSize - 1;
    const focusPos = hasSubList ? insertPos + 2 : insertPos + 3;

    const subTaskNode = {
      type: 'taskItem',
      attrs: { checked: false, priority: 'normal' },
      content: [{ type: 'paragraph' }]
    };

    const contentToInsert = hasSubList
      ? subTaskNode
      : { type: 'taskList', content: [subTaskNode] };

    editor
      .chain()
      .insertContentAt(insertPos, contentToInsert)
      .setTextSelection(focusPos)
      .focus()
      .run();
  };

  const allSubDone = subTotal > 0 && subCompleted === subTotal;

  return (
    <NodeViewWrapper
      as="li"
      data-type="taskItem"
      data-checked={String(checked)}
      data-priority={priority}
      className="priority-task-item group/task my-1"
    >
      <div className="task-item-row flex items-start gap-2 w-full">
        {/* 左侧：方框勾选 + 4级重要度徽章 */}
        <div
          contentEditable={false}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-[3px] shrink-0 select-none relative"
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={!editor.isEditable}
            onClick={handleToggleChecked}
            className={`todo-checkbox todo-check-${priority} ${checked ? 'is-checked' : ''} ${
              !editor.isEditable ? 'cursor-default' : 'cursor-pointer'
            }`}
            title={checked ? '取消完成' : '标记为已完成（仅划掉本行）'}
          >
            {checked && <Check size={11} strokeWidth={3} />}
          </button>

          <div ref={menuRef} className="relative">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={!editor.isEditable}
              onClick={() => editor.isEditable && setMenuOpen((prev) => !prev)}
              className={`todo-prio-badge todo-prio-${priority} ${
                !editor.isEditable ? 'cursor-default' : 'cursor-pointer hover:brightness-110'
              }`}
              title="点击切换待办重要等级（紧急 / 重要 / 普通 / 低）"
            >
              {PRIORITY_LABEL_MAP[priority]}
            </button>

            {menuOpen && (
              <div className="absolute left-0 top-full mt-1 w-24 bg-[#252830] border border-gray-700 rounded-xl shadow-2xl p-1 z-50">
                {TODO_PRIORITY_LIST.map((item) => (
                  <button
                    key={item.key}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleSelectPriority(item.key)}
                    className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] hover:bg-[#323640] text-gray-200 ${
                      priority === item.key ? 'bg-blue-600/20 font-semibold' : ''
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <span
                        className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0"
                        style={{ backgroundColor: item.dotColor }}
                      />
                      <span>{item.label}</span>
                    </span>
                    {priority === item.key && <Check size={11} className="text-blue-400" />}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 中间：待办正文与嵌套子待办列表 */}
        <div className="task-item-body flex-1 min-w-0">
          <NodeViewContent className="task-item-content" />
        </div>

        {/* 右侧：子任务进度计数 (例如 1/3) + 添加子待办按钮 */}
        <div
          contentEditable={false}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-[2px] shrink-0 select-none"
        >
          {subTotal > 0 && (
            <span
              className={`text-[10px] font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border ${
                allSubDone
                  ? 'bg-green-950/50 text-green-400 border-green-700/50'
                  : 'bg-[#1a1c23] text-gray-400 border-gray-700/80'
              }`}
              title={`子待办完成进度：已完成 ${subCompleted} 项，共 ${subTotal} 项`}
            >
              {subCompleted}/{subTotal}
            </span>
          )}

          {editor.isEditable && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleAddSubTask}
              className="opacity-100 md:opacity-0 md:group-hover/task:opacity-100 flex items-center gap-0.5 text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#252830] hover:bg-blue-600/25 text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-300 border border-gray-700/80 transition-all"
              title="在此待办下方添加子待办事项（也可在子行按 Tab 缩进）"
            >
              <ListPlus size={11} />
              <span>子待办</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </NodeViewWrapper>
  );
}

export const PriorityTaskItem = TaskItem.extend({
  addAttributes() {
    return {
      ...this.parent?.(),
      priority: {
        default: 'normal',
        keepOnSplit: false,
        parseHTML: (element: HTMLElement) =>
          element.getAttribute('data-priority') || 'normal',
        renderHTML: (attributes: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
          'data-priority': (attributes.priority as string) || 'normal'
        })
      }
    };
  },

  addNodeView() {
    return ReactNodeViewRenderer(PriorityTaskItemView);
  }
});

// ============================================================================
// 2. 可折叠三主题语法高亮代码窗口节点 (CollapsibleCodeBlock)
// ============================================================================
function CollapsibleCodeBlockView({
  node,
  updateAttributes,
  deleteNode,
  editor
}: NodeViewProps) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [wordWrap, setWordWrap] = useState<boolean>(
    () => localStorage.getItem('notes_code_word_wrap') === 'true'
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const syncWrap = () => {
      setWordWrap(localStorage.getItem('notes_code_word_wrap') === 'true');
    };
    window.addEventListener('notes_word_wrap_change', syncWrap);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('notes_word_wrap_change', syncWrap);
  }, []);

  const code = String(node.attrs.code || '');
  const language = String(node.attrs.language || 'python');
  const title = String(node.attrs.title || '代码片段.py');
  const collapsed = Boolean(node.attrs.collapsed);

  const lineCount = Math.max(1, code.split('\n').length);
  const firstLinePreview =
    code.split('\n').find((l) => l.trim().length > 0) || '空代码块';

  const handleToggleWrap = () => {
    const next = !wordWrap;
    localStorage.setItem('notes_code_word_wrap', String(next));
    setWordWrap(next);
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('notes_word_wrap_change'));
  };

  const handleCopy = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(code);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500);
  };

  return (
    <NodeViewWrapper
      className="my-2.5 select-none"
      data-type="collapsible-code-block"
    >
      <div
        contentEditable={false}
        className="bg-[#13151b] border border-indigo-500/40 rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-md"
      >
        <div className="px-3.5 py-2 bg-[#1c2029] border-b border-gray-800 flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-1 min-w-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => updateAttributes({ collapsed: !collapsed })}
              className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-indigo-300 hover:text-white bg-indigo-600/20 px-2 py-1 rounded-md transition-colors shrink-0"
            >
              {collapsed ? <ChevronDown size={14} /> : <ChevronUp size={14} />}
              <span>{collapsed ? '展开' : '收起'}</span>
            </button>

            <input
              type="text"
              readOnly={!editor.isEditable}
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => updateAttributes({ title: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="代码块说明..."
              className="bg-[#111318] border border-gray-700 rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-gray-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 w-36 shrink-0"
            />

            <select
              disabled={!editor.isEditable}
              value={language}
              onChange={(e) => updateAttributes({ language: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-[#111318] border border-gray-700 rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-blue-300 focus:outline-none shrink-0"
            >
              <option value="python">Python</option>
              <option value="typescript">TypeScript / JS</option>
              <option value="sql">SQL</option>
              <option value="bash">Bash / Shell</option>
              <option value="json">JSON</option>
            </select>

            {collapsed && (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 font-mono truncate ml-2">
                ({lineCount} 行) {firstLinePreview}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleToggleWrap}
              className={`flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] px-2 py-1 rounded border transition-colors ${
                wordWrap
                  ? 'bg-blue-600/25 border-blue-500/50 text-blue-300'
                  : 'bg-[#252830] border-gray-700 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200'
              }`}
              title="切换自动换行"
            >
              <WrapText size={12} />
              <span>换行</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleCopy}
              className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs bg-[#252830] hover:bg-gray-700 text-gray-200 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md border border-gray-700 transition-colors"
            >
              {copied ? <Check size={12} className="text-green-400" /> : <Copy size={12} />}
              <span>{copied ? '已复制' : '复制代码'}</span>
            </button>

            {editor.isEditable && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={deleteNode}
                className="p-1 text-gray-500 hover:text-red-400 transition-colors"
                title="删除此代码块窗口"
              >
                <Trash2 size={14} />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {!collapsed && (
          <div className="p-2 bg-[#111318]">
            <SyntaxHighlightEditor
              value={code}
              readOnly={!editor.isEditable}
              wordWrap={wordWrap}
              onChange={(val) => updateAttributes({ code: val })}
              placeholder="# 在此输入代码块内容..."
              autoHeight
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </NodeViewWrapper>
  );
}

export const CollapsibleCodeBlock = Node.create({
  name: 'collapsibleCodeBlock',
  group: 'block',
  atom: true,
  isolating: true,

  addAttributes() {
    return {
      code: {
        default: '',
        parseHTML: (el) => el.getAttribute('data-code') ?? el.textContent ?? '',
        renderHTML: (attrs) => ({ 'data-code': attrs.code || '' })
      },
      language: {
        default: 'python',
        parseHTML: (el) => el.getAttribute('data-language') || 'python',
        renderHTML: (attrs) => ({ 'data-language': attrs.language || 'python' })
      },
      title: {
        default: '代码片段.py',
        parseHTML: (el) => el.getAttribute('data-title') || '代码片段.py',
        renderHTML: (attrs) => ({ 'data-title': attrs.title || '代码片段.py' })
      },
      collapsed: {
        default: false,
        parseHTML: (el) => el.getAttribute('data-collapsed') === 'true',
        renderHTML: (attrs) => ({ 'data-collapsed': String(Boolean(attrs.collapsed)) })
      }
    };
  },

  parseHTML() {
    return [
      { tag: 'div[data-type="collapsible-code-block"]' },
      {
        tag: 'pre',
        preserveWhitespace: 'full',
        getAttrs: (el) => {
          const preEl = el as HTMLElement;
          const codeEl = preEl.querySelector('code');
          const cls = codeEl?.className || '';
          const langMatch = cls.match(/language-(\w+)/);
          return {
            code: codeEl?.textContent ?? preEl.textContent ?? '',
            language: langMatch ? langMatch[1] : 'python',
            title: '代码片段',
            collapsed: false
          };
        }
      }
    ];
  },

  renderHTML({ HTMLAttributes }) {
    return [
      'div',
      mergeAttributes(HTMLAttributes, { 'data-type': 'collapsible-code-block' })
    ];
  },

  addNodeView() {
    return ReactNodeViewRenderer(CollapsibleCodeBlockView);
  }
});

// ============================================================================
// 3. 可折叠内嵌图片块节点 (CollapsibleImageBlock)
//    - 保留收起为长条模式、缩放 (25/50/75/100%)、复制图片、剪切图片、删除
// ============================================================================
function CollapsibleImageBlockView({
  node,
  updateAttributes,
  deleteNode,
  selected,
  editor
}: NodeViewProps) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const src = String(node.attrs.src || '');
  const title = String(node.attrs.title || 'image.png');
  const width = Number(node.attrs.width) || 75;
  const collapsed = Boolean(node.attrs.collapsed);

  const handleCopyImage = async () => {
    if (!src) return;
    try {
      const response = await fetch(src);
      const blob = await response.blob();
      if (window.ClipboardItem && navigator.clipboard?.write) {
        const pngBlob =
          blob.type === 'image/png' ? blob : new Blob([blob], { type: 'image/png' });
        await navigator.clipboard.write([new ClipboardItem({ 'image/png': pngBlob })]);
      } else {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(`__NOTE_IMG__:${src}`);
      }
    } catch {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(`__NOTE_IMG__:${src}`).catch(() => {});
    }
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1600);
  };

  const handleCutImage = async () => {
    if (!editor.isEditable) return;
    await handleCopyImage();
    deleteNode();
  };

  return (
    <NodeViewWrapper
      className="my-2 select-none"
      data-type="collapsible-image-block"
    >
      <div
        contentEditable={false}
        className={`bg-[#14171f] border rounded-xl overflow-hidden transition-all ${
          selected
            ? 'border-blue-400 ring-2 ring-blue-500/30 shadow-lg'
            : 'border-gray-800/90 hover:border-gray-700'
        }`}
      >
        <div className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-[#1b1f2a] flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0 flex-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => updateAttributes({ collapsed: !collapsed })}
              className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-300 hover:text-white bg-emerald-600/20 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md transition-colors shrink-0"
              title={collapsed ? '展开图片' : '收起图片为长条'}
            >
              {collapsed ? <ChevronDown size={13} /> : <ChevronUp size={13} />}
              <span>{collapsed ? '展开' : '收起'}</span>
            </button>

            <ImageIcon size={13} className="text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />

            <input
              type="text"
              readOnly={!editor.isEditable}
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => updateAttributes({ title: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="图片名称..."
              className="bg-[#111318] border border-gray-700/80 rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-gray-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 w-36 truncate"
            />

            {collapsed ? (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 truncate">
                (已收起图片 · 可直接复制/剪切)
              </span>
            ) : (
              editor.isEditable && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1 ml-1">
                  {[25, 50, 75, 100].map((w) => (
                    <button
                      key={w}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => updateAttributes({ width: w })}
                      className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] ${
                        width === w
                          ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white'
                          : 'bg-[#222631] text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-200'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {w}%
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleCopyImage}
              className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded bg-[#252830] hover:bg-gray-700 text-gray-200 border border-gray-700"
              title="复制图片"
            >
              {copied ? <Check size={12} className="text-green-400" /> : <Copy size={12} />}
              <span>{copied ? '已复制' : '复制图片'}</span>
            </button>

            {editor.isEditable && (
              <>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleCutImage}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded bg-[#252830] hover:bg-gray-700 text-yellow-300 border border-gray-700"
                  title="剪切图片"
                >
                  <Scissors size={12} />
                  <span>剪切</span>
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={deleteNode}
                  className="p-1 text-gray-500 hover:text-red-400"
                  title="删除图片"
                >
                  <Trash2 size={14} />
                </button>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {!collapsed && src && (
          <div className="flex justify-center bg-[#0e1015] p-2.5 border-t border-gray-800/80 overflow-hidden">
            <img
              src={src}
              alt={title}
              style={{ width: `${width}%` }}
              className="max-w-full h-auto rounded object-contain"
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </NodeViewWrapper>
  );
}

export const CollapsibleImageBlock = Node.create({
  name: 'collapsibleImageBlock',
  group: 'block',
  atom: true,

  addAttributes() {
    return {
      src: {
        default: '',
        parseHTML: (el) => el.getAttribute('data-src') || el.getAttribute('src') || '',
        renderHTML: (attrs) => ({ 'data-src': attrs.src || '' })
      },
      title: {
        default: 'image.png',
        parseHTML: (el) => el.getAttribute('data-title') || el.getAttribute('alt') || 'image.png',
        renderHTML: (attrs) => ({ 'data-title': attrs.title || 'image.png' })
      },
      width: {
        default: 75,
        parseHTML: (el) => Number(el.getAttribute('data-width')) || 75,
        renderHTML: (attrs) => ({ 'data-width': String(attrs.width || 75) })
      },
      collapsed: {
        default: false,
        parseHTML: (el) => el.getAttribute('data-collapsed') === 'true',
        renderHTML: (attrs) => ({ 'data-collapsed': String(Boolean(attrs.collapsed)) })
      }
    };
  },

  parseHTML() {
    return [
      { tag: 'div[data-type="collapsible-image-block"]' },
      { tag: 'img[src]' }
    ];
  },

  renderHTML({ HTMLAttributes }) {
    return [
      'div',
      mergeAttributes(HTMLAttributes, { 'data-type': 'collapsible-image-block' })
    ];
  },

  addNodeView() {
    return ReactNodeViewRenderer(CollapsibleImageBlockView);
  }
});
```
